`.env.example`:

```example
# Copy to .env and configure your own SSH destination. .env is Git-ignored.
DECKY_HOST=
DECKY_PORT=22
# Optional key/host-key files; relative paths are resolved from the project root.
DECKY_SSH_IDENTITY=
DECKY_SSH_KNOWN_HOSTS=
# Optional host Python executable and guest Decky Python version override.
PYTHON=
DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION=

`AGENTS.md`:

```md
# AGENTS.md

Notes for an AI coding agent working in this repo. See [README.md](README.md) for what the
plugin does. Project structure, dev loop and conventions are deliberately the same as
[CreamDeck](https://github.com/Qiming-Liu/CreamDeck) — its AGENTS.md has the long-form notes on
on-device testing (CDP on `127.0.0.1:8080`, `pnpm dev`, Big Picture layout gotchas), and they
apply here unchanged.

## Commands

```bash
git submodule update --init
pnpm install
bash scripts/vendor_deps.sh            # Python deps → py_modules/_vendor (git-ignored)
python scripts/build_gcm_core.py        # required before direct backend tests
pnpm run build                         # generates GCM core + rollup → dist/index.js
pnpm run typecheck                     # tsc --noEmit
node --test tests/menu.test.mjs tests/dev.test.mjs         # menu cleanup and optional integration
python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
pnpm dev                               # local build → SSH sync → VM live session
```

Run the typecheck and unit tests after every change. Keep test coverage focused on
authentication, data integrity, installation/shutdown, and menu cleanup. Avoid tests
that duplicate upstream behavior or assert implementation details.

## Architecture

- `main.py` — Decky `Plugin` class, the RPC surface the frontend calls via `callable()`.
- `scripts/build_gcm_core.py` — generates ignored `_gcm_core` modules from explicit
  upstream AST nodes, validating dependencies. Never edit `gcm/` or generated files.
- `py_modules/gcm_bridge.py`, `gcm_runtime.py` — generated-core loading and the narrow
  Decky platform boundary. Do not install fake global `config`/`PyQt6` modules.
- `py_modules/trainer_search.py` — serialized upstream search invocation, detached results
  and Decky IDs; business logic lives in the generated GCM worker.
- `py_modules/http_download.py` — invokes upstream retries/ranges/cache logic; validates
  database JSON before publishing. Preserve archive extraction/installation safeguards.
- `py_modules/trainer_db.py` — local copy of GCM's database JSONs (`<settings>/db/`).
- `py_modules/trainer_download.py` — in-memory jobs; download/extract into same-filesystem
  staging and atomically publish complete installations. Limit concurrent jobs to two;
  shutdown signals cancellation and waits for workers. Metadata is written before the
  final rename; committed jobs remain successful if temporary cleanup fails. Jobs keep
  their original destination; reject stale displayed directories on download requests.
- `py_modules/trainer_library.py` — persistent shared folder setting, existing-file scans,
  per-trainer metadata and App ID/name filtering. Never move/delete files on folder selection.
- `py_modules/trainer_archive.py` — bounded extraction with path/link validation. ZIP uses
  stdlib, 7z/RAR use vendored libarchive-c plus the device's native libarchive.
- `py_modules/steam_library.py`, `vdf_lite.py` — Steam manifest lookup adapted from CreamDeck,
  using Decky's actual user home, including additional Steam libraries.
- `py_modules/data_source.py` — singleton authentication loader and sanitized status/errors.
- `py_modules/gcm_auth_adapter.py` — adapts supplied signer AST without Windows imports.
  Preserve canonical/HMAC/renewal algorithms. `gcm_auth_platform.py` owns private local
  identity/credential storage; `gcm_transport.py` handles TLS and error redaction.
- Raw and generated authentication configurations are Git-ignored. Never print keys,
  credentials, enrollment responses or signed URLs. Tests use the sanitized fixture only.
- `src/hooks/useLibrary.ts`, `useDownloads.ts` — cross-page invalidation and one shared
  download poller. Keep filters and expanded rows local to each page.
- `src/index.tsx` — Quick Access panel (`TrainerSearch`) and the `/game-cheats-manager/:appid`
  route opened from the library context menu (`src/patch.tsx`, from CreamDeck/CheatDeck).

Decky's bundled Python is CPython 3.14 with no pip; third-party packages ship vendored in
`py_modules/_vendor` (built in CI by `scripts/vendor_deps.sh`, never committed).

## Commit style

Concise imperative title (~70 chars), blank line, prose paragraphs explaining *why*.

## VM development

See `scripts/DEV.md`. `scripts/dev.sh` starts the dependency-free Node host watcher;
`dev-session.sh` runs in one remote SSH TTY, owns sudo, symlinks and cleanup. VM
connection settings live in ignored `.env`; private keys and cached Linux
wheels live in ignored `.decky-dev/`. Never store passwords in either location.
The guest source mirror is under `/tmp/decky-gcm-dev-<uid>` to avoid filling its
system partition. Keep the watched `dist` directory stable during synchronization.

## Project language

Use English for all project-authored content, including documentation, code comments,
UI text, error messages, and commit messages. Preserve external identifiers and data
when their exact values are required for correct behavior.

## Steam / Decky CDP debugging

### Connection

The user-provided topology exposes Steam's guest-loopback CDP endpoint through an
SSH local forward:

```text
Host 127.0.0.1:18080 -> SSH -> Guest 127.0.0.1:8080
```

Reuse the existing tunnel. No VirtualBox port-forwarding change is needed. If the
tunnel must be recreated, use the existing SSH host configuration and authentication:

```powershell
# Replace the placeholder with the existing SSH host alias before running.
ssh -N -o ExitOnForwardFailure=yes -L 127.0.0.1:18080:127.0.0.1:8080 <existing-ssh-host>
```

If the local port is already listening, inspect the existing connection before
starting another tunnel. Do not close the user's existing tunnel when finished.

### Inspect the endpoint and targets

Run on the Windows host in PowerShell:

```powershell
Invoke-RestMethod 'http://127.0.0.1:18080/json/version' -TimeoutSec 8
$targets = Invoke-RestMethod 'http://127.0.0.1:18080/json/list' -TimeoutSec 8
$targets | ForEach-Object {
    $_ | Select-Object id, title, type, url, webSocketDebuggerUrl
}
netstat -ano -p tcp | Select-String ':18080'
```

Use the current `webSocketDebuggerUrl` from `/json/list`; do not cache target IDs.
If it advertises the guest address, replace only the WebSocket host and port with
`127.0.0.1:18080`, preserving the path and target ID.

| Target | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `SharedJSContext` | Steam's shared JS context; inspect Decky globals and loaded plugins |
| Steam Big Picture (localized title) | Big Picture page and screenshots |
| `QuickAccess_uid2` | Quick Access menu |
| `MainMenu_uid2` | Main menu |
| `notificationtoasts_uid2` | Notification popups |

Copy localized titles from the current target list when selecting a target. Do not
infer page content from its URL alone: Big Picture and popup targets may use
`about:blank?...` URLs.

### Read-only JavaScript checks

Define this function in host PowerShell. It uses .NET without external packages:

```powershell
function Invoke-SteamCdpRead {
    param([string]$Title='SharedJSContext', [string]$Expression)
    $ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
    $targets=Invoke-RestMethod 'http://127.0.0.1:18080/json/list' -TimeoutSec 10
    $target=@($targets | Where-Object title -eq $Title)[0]
    if (!$target) { throw "Target missing: $Title" }
    $ws=[System.Net.WebSockets.ClientWebSocket]::new()
    $cts=[System.Threading.CancellationTokenSource]::new(15000)
    try {
        [void]$ws.ConnectAsync([Uri]$target.webSocketDebuggerUrl,$cts.Token).GetAwaiter().GetResult()
        $msg=@{id=1;method='Runtime.evaluate';params=@{
            expression=$Expression;returnByValue=$true;throwOnSideEffect=$true;timeout=5000
        }} | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 12 -Compress
        $bytes=[Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($msg)
        [void]$ws.SendAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($bytes),[System.Net.WebSockets.WebSocketMessageType]::Text,$true,$cts.Token).GetAwaiter().GetResult()
        do {
            $stream=[IO.MemoryStream]::new()
            do {
                $buffer=New-Object byte[] 65536
                $received=$ws.ReceiveAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($buffer),$cts.Token).GetAwaiter().GetResult()
                $stream.Write($buffer,0,$received.Count)
            } until ($received.EndOfMessage)
            $response=[Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetString($stream.ToArray()) | ConvertFrom-Json
            $stream.Dispose()
        } until ($response.id -eq 1)
        $response | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 30
    } finally { $ws.Dispose(); $cts.Dispose() }
}

Invoke-SteamCdpRead -Expression 'JSON.stringify({title:document.title,deckyHasLoaded:globalThis.deckyHasLoaded})'
Invoke-SteamCdpRead -Expression 'JSON.stringify(DeckyPluginLoader.plugins.map(p=>({name:p.name,version:p.version})))'
```

The default target is `SharedJSContext`. Use `-Title` for another title taken from
the current target list. The function sends this CDP request:

```json
{
  "id": 1,
  "method": "Runtime.evaluate",
  "params": {
    "expression": "JSON.stringify({deckyHasLoaded:globalThis.deckyHasLoaded})",
    "returnByValue": true,
    "throwOnSideEffect": true,
    "timeout": 5000
  }
}
```

Match responses by `id`, assemble WebSocket fragments through `EndOfMessage`, then
parse JSON. Inspect the top-level `error` and `result.exceptionDetails`; receiving
a response does not prove evaluation succeeded. String results from the expressions
above are in `result.result.value`.

Keep object inspection narrow:

```javascript
Object.keys(globalThis).filter(k => /decky|plugin/i.test(k))
Object.keys(DeckyPluginLoader)
DeckyPluginLoader.plugins.map(p => ({name:p.name,version:p.version}))
```

Do not dump authentication data. In particular, do not read or print
`deckyAuthToken`, complete backend connection objects, storage values, or credentials.

### Screenshots, console, network, and React

The following are follow-up debugging methods; they were not all exercised during
the verification below. `Invoke-SteamCdpRead` supports only `Runtime.evaluate`.
Use a separate CDP client or extend the helper for other methods.

- Screenshots: connect to the Big Picture target and send `Page.captureScreenshot`
  with `{"format":"png"}`. Decode `result.data` from Base64 to PNG. A screenshot
  does not require `Page.bringToFront`, navigation, or clicks.
- Console: send `Runtime.enable` on the selected target and listen for
  `Runtime.consoleAPICalled` and `Runtime.exceptionThrown`. Capture coverage begins
  when attached; historical logs are not guaranteed.
- Network: send `Network.enable` and listen for `Network.requestWillBeSent`,
  `Network.responseReceived`, and `Network.loadingFailed`. Record relevant traffic
  only; avoid exporting authentication headers or sensitive response bodies. Do not
  automatically reload the page to collect traffic.
- React: identify the target containing the UI, then inspect the relevant DOM
  node's React fiber properties or an existing `__REACT_DEVTOOLS_GLOBAL_HOOK__`.
  Internal properties vary by version. Do not assume fixed fiber fields or directly
  modify props/state during read-only inspection.
- UI actions: use `Input.dispatchMouseEvent` or `Input.dispatchKeyEvent` when needed
  for reproduction. First record the current screen, target, and expected result.
  Read-only verification does not click, navigate, or reload plugins.

Close CDP WebSockets created for the investigation when finished. Leave Steam and
the user's existing SSH connection running.

### Verified snapshot (2026-10-31)

These observations describe that verification session. Re-query them in later
tasks rather than treating them as permanent configuration:

- Host `127.0.0.1:18080` was reachable; its listening PID belonged to `ssh`.
- `/json/version` reported `Chrome/126.0.6478.183`, protocol `1.3`, and a user agent
  containing `Valve Steam Client`.
- `/json/list` returned six targets: the five categories above and a
  `data:text/html,...` page.
- Read-only JavaScript executed through WebSocket in `SharedJSContext` confirmed
  `deckyHasLoaded === true`.
- `DeckyPluginLoader.plugins` returned `Decky Game Cheats Manager`, version `0.1.1`.
- The earlier name/version, `Game Cheats Manager` / `0.1.0-dev.20261031.1`, no longer
  described the loaded plugin at verification time.
- The historical screenshot at
  `C:\Users\Administrator\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\steam_vm.png` existed and
  displayed the Steam Big Picture home screen. It is not a live view.
- Verification did not modify the UI, deploy the plugin, or close the tunnel.

Host-side CDP responses alone do not independently establish that the guest listens
only on loopback, the exact SSH forwarding destination, whether VirtualBox settings
were previously changed, or which command deployed the plugin. Inspect guest
listeners, SSH launch arguments, and actual VM configuration when those facts matter.

### Troubleshooting

- Connection refused: check the `18080` listener and existing tunnel before checking
  guest Steam/CDP. Do not immediately change VirtualBox configuration.
- Stale WebSocket target: fetch `/json/list` again and select by current title.
- Missing Decky globals: confirm `SharedJSContext`, check whether loading is still
  in progress, and report the actual exception.
- Unexpected plugin name/version: use runtime `DeckyPluginLoader.plugins` as the
  evidence. Local source, a successful build, or running a deployment command does
  not establish that Steam loaded that build.
- `throwOnSideEffect` rejected an expression: reduce it to simple field reads.
  Do not automatically remove this restriction for read-only verification.
- PowerShell target arrays: expand with `ForEach-Object` before `Select-Object` to
  avoid selecting fields from the array itself and producing null values.
- During verification, the host `node` command had no active version and `python`
  was unavailable. Prefer PowerShell/.NET for basic CDP checks instead of installing
  unrelated dependencies.

```

`README.md`:

```md
# DeckyGCM

[![Build](https://img.shields.io/github/actions/workflow/status/Qiming-Liu/Decky-Game-Cheats-Manager/pr-build-test.yml?branch=main&label=build)](https://github.com/Qiming-Liu/Decky-Game-Cheats-Manager/actions/workflows/pr-build-test.yml)
[![License: GPL 3.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-GPL--3.0-blue)](./LICENSE)

A [Decky Loader](https://github.com/SteamDeckHomebrew/decky-loader) plugin for managing
and downloading game trainers on Steam Deck, powered by
[Game Cheats Manager](https://github.com/dyang886/Game-Cheats-Manager) (GCM).

## Features

- **Per-game workspace**: use the left navigation to search, manage downloads, or
  bind a local trainer. Search starts with the game's name.
- **Folder selection**: choose where to keep your trainers. The folder is shared by
  the Quick Access panel and game pages, and your choice is remembered across restarts.
- **Online search**: find trainers from FLiNG, XiaoXing, Cheat Tables and GCM using
  English or Chinese game names, including pinyin and cross-language matching.
- **Download and extract**: follow download and extraction progress, with up to two
  downloads running at once. Completed trainers appear in the local library.
  Existing files are preserved if a download or installation fails.
- **Game context menu**: open **DeckyGCM** from a game's library context
  menu to see its local trainers and automatically search for more.
- **Game associations**: trainers downloaded from a game page stay associated with
  that game's Steam App ID. Existing trainers are matched by game name.
- **Sidecar programs**: select a downloaded or imported `.exe` or `.bat` to start
  alongside its game through Proton, using Steam's per-game launch options.
- **Local imports**: copy a selected file into the trainer folder and bind the copy
  to the game's App ID. Original files are preserved.
- **Download management**: cancel active jobs and delete plugin-managed trainers
  after confirmation. Other game associations and existing folders are protected.
- **Database refresh**: update the trainer catalog from inside the plugin. Once
  downloaded, the catalog can be searched offline.
- **Steam language detection**: settings, game pages, notifications and confirmation
  dialogs follow Steam's English, Simplified Chinese or Traditional Chinese interface
  language. Other languages fall back to English.

## Using the plugin

The Quick Access panel contains only **Settings**: choose the trainer folder and
update the trainer catalog. The default folder is `~/GCM Trainers/`.

Open **DeckyGCM** from a game's library context menu. The page
uses Decky's native left navigation, with three sections:

1. **Search**: edit the prefilled game name, search the catalog and download trainers.
2. **Downloaded**: view progress, cancel downloads, and expand a trainer's **Manage**
   actions to select its sidecar executable or delete it. Use **Disable sidecar**
   before deleting the currently selected program.
3. **Bind trainer**: choose an `.exe`, `.bat` or `.CT` file and select **Copy and bind**.
   The copied file appears under Downloaded. Only the selected file is copied;
   dependencies beside the original are not imported automatically.

Sidecar changes apply when the game next starts through Proton. Cheat Tables need
Cheat Engine and cannot be selected as executable sidecars. Existing launch options
are preserved; unsupported shell syntax prevents editing. Shared filesystem entries
remain on disabling a sidecar so other launch tools can continue using them.

Changing folders does not move existing files or change existing sidecar paths.
Active downloads finish in their original destination, which stays visible in the
game's download activity. Only the currently selected folder is scanned for trainers.
Deletion removes plugin-managed trainer folders or individual trainer files; existing
unmanaged folders and trainers shared with other games cannot be deleted here.

## Building from GCM

The UI uses the existing Decky backend. The pinned `gcm/` submodule remains the
source of the search/download core; `pnpm run build` automatically runs
`scripts/build_gcm_core.py` before Rollup. No upstream GCM or generated-core files
need to be edited for this UI.

```bash
git submodule update --init
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
pnpm run typecheck
pnpm run build
bash scripts/vendor_deps.sh
node --test tests/menu.test.mjs tests/dev.test.mjs tests/sidecar.test.mjs tests/i18n.test.mjs
python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
```

Build without authentication for local development, or provide the existing
`GCM_SECRET_CONFIG_PATH` / release workflow secret for an authenticated build.
An unauthenticated build still succeeds, but real catalog/trainer downloads require
the configured GCM data source. The existing CI packaging workflow is unchanged.

## License

GPL-3.0, same as upstream Game Cheats Manager.

```

`main.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
import sys

sys.path.append(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "py_modules"))

import gcm_bridge

gcm_bridge.ensure_paths()

import action_log  # noqa: E402
import decky  # noqa: E402
import trainer_db  # noqa: E402
import trainer_download  # noqa: E402
import trainer_search  # noqa: E402
import trainer_library  # noqa: E402
import steam_library  # noqa: E402


class Plugin:
    # A bounded cache shared by both panels: searching another game must not
    # invalidate the first panel's download buttons. Only cached IDs are accepted.
    _last_results: dict[str, dict] = {}

    @action_log.log_call
    async def get_database_status(self):
        return trainer_db.status()

    @action_log.log_call
    async def refresh_database(self):
        return await asyncio.to_thread(trainer_db.refresh)

    @action_log.log_call
    async def search_trainers(self, keyword: str, lang: str = "en"):
        try:
            results = await asyncio.to_thread(trainer_search.search, keyword, "zh" if lang == "zh" else "en")
        except trainer_search.SearchError as e:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(e), "results": []}
        Plugin._last_results.update({t["id"]: t for t in results})
        while len(Plugin._last_results) > 2000:
            del Plugin._last_results[next(iter(Plugin._last_results))]
        return {
            "success": True,
            "results": [
                {k: t[k] for k in ("id", "trainer_name", "game_name", "origin", "version")}
                for t in results
            ],
        }

    @action_log.log_call
    async def download_trainer(self, trainer_id: str, app_id: str = "", expected_directory: str = ""):
        if app_id and (not app_id.isascii() or not app_id.isdigit()):
            return {"success": False, "error": "invalid_app_id"}
        trainer = Plugin._last_results.get(trainer_id)
        if trainer is None:
            return {"success": False, "error": "unknown_trainer"}
        try:
            job = trainer_download.start(trainer, app_id=app_id, expected_directory=expected_directory)
            return {"success": True, "job": job}
        except ValueError as error:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(error)}

    async def get_downloads(self):
        # Polled by the UI while a download runs — deliberately not logged.
        return trainer_download.jobs()

    @action_log.log_call
    async def get_library(self, app_id: str = "", game_name: str = ""):
        try:
            if app_id:
                if not app_id.isascii() or not app_id.isdigit():
                    return {"success": False, "error": "invalid_app_id"}
                game = await asyncio.to_thread(steam_library.find_game, app_id)
                game_name = game["name"] if game else game_name
            library = await asyncio.to_thread(trainer_library.scan, app_id, game_name)
            return {"success": True, "game_name": game_name, **library}
        except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(e), "directory": trainer_library.directory()}

    @action_log.log_call
    async def set_library_directory(self, path: str):
        try:
            directory = await asyncio.to_thread(trainer_library.set_directory, path)
            return {"success": True, "directory": directory}
        except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}

    async def get_library_settings(self):
        return {"directory": trainer_library.directory(), "database": trainer_db.status()}

    async def manage_trainer(self, action: str, app_id: str, expected_directory: str,
                             entry_id: str = "", filename: str = "", source: str = "", game_name: str = ""):
        if not app_id or not app_id.isascii() or not app_id.isdigit():
            return {"success": False, "error": "invalid_app_id"}
        try:
            if action == "import":
                result = await asyncio.to_thread(trainer_library.import_file, source, app_id, game_name, expected_directory)
            elif action == "delete":
                await asyncio.to_thread(trainer_library.delete, entry_id, expected_directory, app_id)
                result = {}
            elif action == "executable":
                result = {"path": await asyncio.to_thread(trainer_library.executable, entry_id, filename, expected_directory)}
            elif action == "cancel":
                result = {"job": trainer_download.cancel(entry_id, app_id)}
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown trainer action.")
            return {"success": True, **result}
        except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(error)}

    async def _main(self):
        trainer_download.resume()
        decky.logger.info("DeckyGCM loaded")

    async def _unload(self):
        await trainer_download.shutdown()
        decky.logger.info("DeckyGCM unloaded")

    async def _uninstall(self):
        pass

```

`package.json`:

```json
{
  "name": "decky-game-cheats-manager",
  "version": "0.1.1",
  "description": "Decky Loader plugin bringing Game Cheats Manager's trainer search and download to Steam Deck.",
  "type": "module",
  "scripts": {
    "build": "rollup -c",
    "watch": "rollup -c -w",
    "dev": "bash scripts/dev.sh",

`py_modules/action_log.py`:

```py
"""
A dedicated, persistent action log for this plugin — separate from decky's own
per-session log files (which get replaced fresh every plugin reload, at
DECKY_PLUGIN_LOG_DIR/<timestamp>.log). This one accumulates across restarts
under a fixed filename, specifically so a bug report can be answered with one
file that has "everything the plugin has done" — every Plugin RPC call (args,
outcome, timing) plus notable non-RPC events like trainer-database refreshes —
and the version line at the top of each run makes clear which build produced it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import functools
import logging
import logging.handlers
import os
import time
from typing import Any, Callable

import decky

_LOG_FILE_NAME = "gcm_actions.log"
_MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024
_BACKUP_COUNT = 2

_logger: logging.Logger | None = None


def _get_logger() -> logging.Logger:
    global _logger
    if _logger is not None:
        return _logger

    logger = logging.getLogger("gcm.actions")
    logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    logger.propagate = False  # this is a separate log, not a mirror of decky's own

    log_path = os.path.join(decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_LOG_DIR, _LOG_FILE_NAME)
    handler = logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler(
        log_path, maxBytes=_MAX_BYTES, backupCount=_BACKUP_COUNT, encoding="utf-8"
    )
    handler.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s"))
    logger.addHandler(handler)

    logger.info(f"===== Decky Game Cheats Manager v{decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_VERSION} starting =====")
    _logger = logger
    return logger


def log_call(func: Callable) -> Callable:
    """Decorator for a Plugin async method — logs the call going in (name + args)
    and either its result or its exception coming out, with timing. Re-raises
    whatever it caught, so callers still handle failures exactly as before."""

    @functools.wraps(func)
    async def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
        logger = _get_logger()
        logger.info(f"{func.__name__} called: args={args!r} kwargs={kwargs!r}")
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            result = await func(self, *args, **kwargs)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - log then re-raise, callers still handle it
            elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
            logger.error(f"{func.__name__} raised after {elapsed_ms}ms: {e!r}")
            raise
        elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
        logger.info(f"{func.__name__} returned after {elapsed_ms}ms: {_summarize(result)}")
        return result

    return wrapper


def log_event(message: str) -> None:
    """For notable things that aren't a Plugin RPC call itself — e.g. install-state
    writes, integrity-check mismatches."""
    _get_logger().info(message)


def log_warning(message: str) -> None:
    _get_logger().warning(message)


def _summarize(result: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(result, dict):
        # Full payloads (dlcs/unlockers lists) would bloat every line — only the
        # top-level shape is what actually matters for debugging a call.
        return "{" + ", ".join(f"{k}={_short(v)}" for k, v in result.items()) + "}"
    return _short(result)


def _short(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, (list, dict, set, tuple)):
        return f"<{type(value).__name__} len={len(value)}>"
    return repr(value)

```

`py_modules/data_source.py`:

```py
"""One process-wide GCM signer, with structured errors safe for RPC/logs."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import os
import threading

import decky

SECRET_CONFIG_PATHS = (
    os.path.join(decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_SETTINGS_DIR, "secret_config.py"),
    os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "secret_config.py"),
)
_lock = threading.RLock()
_client = None
_last_status = "unconfigured"
BUNDLED_AUTH_MODULE = "_gcm_core.auth"


class DataSourceUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, message, code="unconfigured"):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def reset():
    """Reload explicitly after configuration changes (also isolates tests)."""
    global _client, _last_status
    with _lock:
        _client, _last_status = None, "unconfigured"


def client():
    global _client, _last_status
    with _lock:
        if _client is not None:
            return _client
        path = next((p for p in SECRET_CONFIG_PATHS if os.path.isfile(p)), None)
        try:
            if path:
                from gcm_auth_adapter import load
                module = load(path)
            else:
                try:
                    module = importlib.import_module(BUNDLED_AUTH_MODULE)
                except ModuleNotFoundError as error:
                    if error.name in {"_gcm_core", BUNDLED_AUTH_MODULE}:
                        raise DataSourceUnavailable("No GCM data source configured", "unconfigured") from None
                    raise
            if not all(getattr(module, name, None) for name in ("CLIENT_API_KEY", "ENROLL_ENDPOINT", "SIGNED_URL_DOWNLOAD_ENDPOINT")) or not callable(getattr(module, "signed_get", None)):
                raise ValueError("Incomplete configuration")
        except DataSourceUnavailable:
            _last_status = "unconfigured"
            raise
        except Exception:
            _last_status = "invalid_config"
            raise DataSourceUnavailable("Could not load GCM authentication configuration", "invalid_config") from None
        _client, _last_status = module, "ready"
        return module


def status():
    try:
        client()
    except DataSourceUnavailable:
        pass
    return {"configured": _client is not None, "state": _last_status}


def is_configured():
    return status()["configured"]


def get_signed_download_url(path_on_s3: str) -> str:
    global _last_status
    client()
    import gcm_transport
    gcm_transport.clear_failure()
    from _gcm_core.download import DownloadBaseThread
    try:
        result = DownloadBaseThread.get_signed_download_url(path_on_s3, raise_errors=True)
        if not isinstance(result, str) or not result.startswith("https://"):
            raise DataSourceUnavailable("Server returned no valid download URL", "invalid_response")
        _last_status = "ready"
        return result
    except DataSourceUnavailable as error:
        _last_status = error.code
        raise
    except Exception as error:
        response = getattr(error, "response", None)
        status_code = getattr(response, "status_code", None)
        previous_failure = gcm_transport.last_failure()
        if previous_failure and previous_failure[0] == "http":
            status_code = status_code or previous_failure[1]
        if status_code == 429:
            code, message = "rate_limited", "GCM server rate limit reached"
        elif isinstance(error, (gcm_transport.ConnectionError, gcm_transport.Timeout)) or (previous_failure and previous_failure[0] == "connection"):
            code, message = "connection_failed", "Could not connect to GCM server"
        elif status_code and status_code >= 500:
            code, message = "server_error", "GCM server request failed"
        elif isinstance(error, RuntimeError) and _last_status in {"connection_failed", "server_error", "rate_limited"}:
            code = _last_status
            message = "GCM enrollment is waiting before retrying"
        elif status_code in (400, 401, 403) or isinstance(error, RuntimeError):
            code, message = "auth_failed", "GCM device authentication failed"
        else:
            code, message = "server_error", "GCM server request failed"
        _last_status = code
        raise DataSourceUnavailable(message, code) from None

```

`py_modules/gcm_auth_adapter.py`:

```py
"""Adapt the supplied GCM signer without importing its Windows initialization."""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import copy
import types
from pathlib import Path

FUNCTIONS = {
    "_enroll", "_get_credential", "_refresh_credential", "_client_meta",
    "_canonical_string", "_signed_headers", "signed_get",
}
CONSTANTS = {
    "ENROLL_ENDPOINT", "SIGNED_URL_DOWNLOAD_ENDPOINT", "SIGNED_URL_UPLOAD_ENDPOINT",
    "VERSION_CHECKER_ENDPOINT", "PATCH_PATTERNS_ENDPOINT", "CLIENT_API_KEY",
    "_ENROLL_TIMEOUT", "_RETRY_INTERVAL", "_UNKNOWN_CREDENTIAL", "_REQUEST_ATTEMPTS",
}


class Adapt(ast.NodeTransformer):
    def visit_ImportFrom(self, node):
        if node.module == "config":
            node.module = "gcm_runtime"
        return node

    def visit_Dict(self, node):
        # The only protocol-field change: describe the actual target platform.
        for key, value in zip(node.keys, node.values):
            if isinstance(key, ast.Constant) and key.value == "platform":
                if not isinstance(value, ast.Constant) or value.value != "win32":
                    raise ValueError("Unsupported GCM enrollment platform schema")
                value.value = "linux"
        return self.generic_visit(node)


def render(source: str) -> str:
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(source)
    except SyntaxError:
        raise ValueError("Invalid GCM secret configuration syntax") from None
    selected = []
    found_functions = set()
    found_constants = set()
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name in FUNCTIONS:
            found_functions.add(node.name)
            selected.append(Adapt().visit(copy.deepcopy(node)))
        elif isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and len(node.targets) == 1 and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name) and node.targets[0].id in CONSTANTS:
            name = node.targets[0].id
            ast.literal_eval(node.value)  # Configuration constants must be literal data.
            found_constants.add(name)
            selected.append(node)
    if found_functions != FUNCTIONS or found_constants != CONSTANTS:
        raise ValueError("Unsupported GCM secret configuration interface")
    preamble = '''import hashlib, hmac, json, os, random, threading, time, uuid
from urllib.parse import urlparse
import gcm_transport as requests
from gcm_auth_platform import credential_path, machine_hash as _machine_hash, store_credential as _store_credential, read_credential as _read_credential
from gcm_runtime import quiet as print
_CREDENTIAL_FILE = credential_path()
_lock = threading.Lock()
_credential = None
_last_attempt = 0.0
'''
    return preamble + ast.unparse(ast.fix_missing_locations(ast.Module(body=selected, type_ignores=[]))) + "\n"


def load(path: str):
    module = types.ModuleType("decky_gcm_private_auth")
    # Compiled filename deliberately does not include the supplied secret's path.
    code = compile(render(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")), "<GCM authentication>", "exec")
    exec(code, module.__dict__)
    return module

```

`py_modules/gcm_auth_platform.py`:

```py
"""SteamOS persistence for GCM device enrollment; no Windows APIs."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import tempfile
import threading
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

import decky

_identity_lock = threading.Lock()


def credential_path():
    return str(Path(decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_SETTINGS_DIR) / "auth" / "credential.json")


def _write_private(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
    os.chmod(path.parent, 0o700)
    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".gcm-", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
            json.dump(value, out)
            out.flush()
            os.fsync(out.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
        os.chmod(path, 0o600)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)


def machine_hash():
    # Stable per installation. No raw hardware identifier leaves the device.
    path = Path(credential_path()).with_name("identity.json")
    with _identity_lock:
        try:
            identity = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["id"]
            uuid.UUID(identity)
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
            identity = uuid.uuid4().hex
            _write_private(path, {"id": identity})
    return hashlib.sha256(f"decky-gcm:{identity}".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]


def store_credential(credential):
    _write_private(credential_path(), credential)


def read_credential():
    try:
        path = Path(credential_path())
        value = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if isinstance(value, dict) and all(isinstance(value.get(k), str) and value[k] for k in ("client_id", "client_secret")):
            os.chmod(path, 0o600)
            return value
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        pass
    return None

```

`py_modules/gcm_bridge.py`:

```py
"""Load the build-generated GCM core without global config/PyQt shims."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys

import decky

PLUGIN_DIR = decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_DIR
VENDOR_DIR = os.path.join(PLUGIN_DIR, "py_modules", "_vendor")
DATABASE_PATH = os.path.join(decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_SETTINGS_DIR, "db")


def ensure_paths():
    if os.path.isdir(VENDOR_DIR) and VENDOR_DIR not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, VENDOR_DIR)


def load_search_index():
    ensure_paths()
    os.makedirs(DATABASE_PATH, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        from _gcm_core import search_index
    except ModuleNotFoundError as error:
        if error.name == "_gcm_core":
            raise RuntimeError("GCM core missing; run scripts/build_gcm_core.py before starting the plugin") from None
        raise
    return search_index


def is_chinese(text):
    from gcm_runtime import is_chinese as detect
    return detect(text)

`py_modules/gcm_runtime.py`:

```py
"""The small platform boundary used by generated upstream GCM modules."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import threading

import decky

DATABASE_PATH = os.path.join(decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_SETTINGS_DIR, "db")
SEARCH_LOCK = threading.RLock()
settings = {
    "language": "en_US", "enSearchResults": False, "sortByOrigin": False,
    "enableFling": True, "enableXiaoXing": True, "enableCT": True, "enableGCM": True,
}


def quiet(*args, **kwargs):
    # Upstream diagnostics can include signed URLs and registration responses.
    # Public adapters surface structured, sanitized errors instead.
    pass


def tr(message):
    return message


def is_chinese(text):
    import zhon.cedict
    return any(char in zhon.cedict.all for char in text)


class Signal:
    def __init__(self, callback=None):
        self.callback = callback
        self.events = []

    def emit(self, *args):
        self.events.append(args)
        if self.callback:
            self.callback(*args)


class BackendWorker:
    """Synchronous event callbacks, with no Qt application/thread dependency."""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        self.message = Signal()
        self.messageBox = Signal()
        self.progress = Signal()
        self.finished = Signal()


class AuthProxy:
    def __getattr__(self, name):
        from data_source import client
        return getattr(client(), name)


auth = AuthProxy()


def __getattr__(name):
    if name in {"APP_VERSION", "API_TIMEOUT", "RETRY_STATUSES"}:
        from _gcm_core import config
        return getattr(config, name)
    raise AttributeError(name)

`py_modules/gcm_transport.py`:

```py
"""requests boundary: retain upstream retries, redact URLs from failures."""
from __future__ import annotations

import certifi
import requests as _requests
import threading

ConnectionError = _requests.ConnectionError
Timeout = _requests.Timeout
_state = threading.local()


def clear_failure():
    _state.failure = None


def last_failure():
    return getattr(_state, "failure", None)


class Response:
    def __init__(self, response):
        self._response = response

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        return getattr(self._response, name)

    def raise_for_status(self):
        if 400 <= self.status_code:
            _state.failure = ("http", self.status_code)
            raise _requests.HTTPError(f"HTTP {self.status_code}", response=self) from None

    def json(self):
        try:
            return self._response.json()
        except ValueError:
            raise ValueError("Server returned invalid JSON") from None

    def iter_content(self, *args, **kwargs):
        try:
            yield from self._response.iter_content(*args, **kwargs)
        except _requests.RequestException:
            raise ConnectionError("Download stream interrupted") from None


class Session(_requests.Session):
    def request(self, *args, **kwargs):
        kwargs.setdefault("verify", certifi.where())
        try:
            return Response(super().request(*args, **kwargs))
        except _requests.Timeout:
            _state.failure = ("connection", None)
            raise Timeout("Server request timed out") from None
        except _requests.RequestException:
            _state.failure = ("connection", None)
            raise ConnectionError("Could not connect to server") from None


def get(url, **kwargs):
    # A non-streaming response has already been read before this session closes.
    with Session() as session:
        return session.get(url, **kwargs)

```

`py_modules/http_download.py`:

```py
"""Thin wrapper around GCM's original retry/range/cache download engine."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path


def download(url, directory, on_progress=None, filename=None):
    from _gcm_core.download import DownloadBaseThread
    from gcm_runtime import Signal
    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)
    worker = DownloadBaseThread()
    if on_progress:
        worker.progress = Signal(lambda segments: on_progress(sum(s[0] for s in segments), sum(s[1] for s in segments)))
    if filename is None:
        result = worker.request_download(url, directory, raise_errors=True, atomic=True)
        if not result:
            raise OSError("GCM download failed verification")
        return result
    # Fixed database filenames must not depend on the server's Content-Disposition.
    # Copy the old cache to staging so upstream ETag/304 handling still works.
    import shutil
    from urllib.parse import unquote_plus, urlparse
    destination = Path(directory) / os.path.basename(filename)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix=".gcm-db-", dir=directory) as staging:
        cached_name = os.path.basename(unquote_plus(urlparse(url).path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]))
        if destination.is_file() and cached_name:
            shutil.copyfile(destination, Path(staging) / cached_name)
        result = worker.request_download(url, staging, raise_errors=True, atomic=True)
        if not result:
            raise OSError("GCM download failed verification")
        # A bad response must not replace a previously working database.
        import json
        with open(result, encoding="utf-8") as source:
            data = json.load(source)
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise ValueError("Invalid trainer database format")
        os.replace(result, destination)
    return str(destination)

```

`py_modules/steam_library.py`:

```py
"""
Port of CreamInstaller/Platforms/Steam/SteamLibrary.cs, scoped to a single
appid instead of enumerating the whole library (per the plugin's per-game
navigation flow: the game is already chosen via the library context menu,
so there is no need to scan every installed game up front).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Optional, TypedDict

import decky

from vdf_lite import get_child, parse

DEFAULT_STEAM_ROOT_CANDIDATES = [
    os.path.join(decky.DECKY_USER_HOME, ".local/share/Steam"),
    os.path.join(decky.DECKY_USER_HOME, ".steam/steam"),
    os.path.join(decky.DECKY_USER_HOME, ".steam/root"),
]


class GameInfo(TypedDict):
    app_id: str
    name: str
    branch: str
    build_id: int
    game_directory: str


def _steam_root() -> Optional[str]:
    for candidate in DEFAULT_STEAM_ROOT_CANDIDATES:
        if os.path.isdir(candidate):
            return candidate
    return None


def _library_folders() -> list[str]:
    """Returns every steamapps folder (default + any additional Steam library, e.g. on an SD card)."""
    root = _steam_root()
    if root is None:
        return []

    folders: list[str] = []
    default_steamapps = os.path.join(root, "steamapps")
    if os.path.isdir(default_steamapps):
        folders.append(default_steamapps)

    library_folders_vdf = os.path.join(default_steamapps, "libraryfolders.vdf")
    if os.path.isfile(library_folders_vdf):
        with open(library_folders_vdf, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            data = parse(f.read())
        root_obj = get_child(data, "libraryfolders")
        if isinstance(root_obj, dict):
            for key, entry in root_obj.items():
                if not key.isdigit() or not isinstance(entry, dict):
                    continue
                raw_path = get_child(entry, "path")
                if not raw_path:
                    continue
                steamapps_path = os.path.join(raw_path, "steamapps")
                if os.path.isdir(steamapps_path) and steamapps_path not in folders:
                    folders.append(steamapps_path)
    return folders


def find_game(app_id: str) -> Optional[GameInfo]:
    """Looks for appmanifest_<app_id>.acf across every known library folder."""
    for library_dir in _library_folders():
        manifest_path = os.path.join(library_dir, f"appmanifest_{app_id}.acf")
        if not os.path.isfile(manifest_path):
            continue
        with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            data = parse(f.read())
        app_state = get_child(data, "AppState")
        if not isinstance(app_state, dict):
            continue

        install_dir = get_child(app_state, "installdir")
        name = get_child(app_state, "name")
        build_id = get_child(app_state, "buildid")
        if not install_dir or not name or not build_id:
            continue

        branch = get_child(app_state, "UserConfig", "BetaKey") or get_child(
            app_state, "MountedConfig", "BetaKey"
        ) or "public"

        game_directory = os.path.join(library_dir, "common", install_dir)
        if not os.path.isdir(game_directory):
            continue

        try:
            build_id_int = int(build_id)
        except ValueError:
            build_id_int = 0

        return GameInfo(
            app_id=app_id,
            name=name,
            branch=branch,
            build_id=build_id_int,
            game_directory=game_directory,
        )
    return None

```

`py_modules/trainer_archive.py`:

```py
"""Extract into private staging; never write archive paths directly to the library."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import stat
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

MAX_FILES = 20000
MAX_BYTES = 8 * 1024**3
ARCHIVE_EXTENSIONS = {".zip", ".7z", ".rar"}


def _target(root: Path, name: str) -> Path:
    name = name.replace("\\", "/")
    parts = name.split("/")
    if name.startswith("/") or any(p == ".." or ":" in p or "\x00" in p for p in parts):
        raise ValueError("Unsafe path in trainer archive.")
    if re.match(r"^[a-zA-Z]:", name):
        raise ValueError("Unsafe path in trainer archive.")
    target = root.joinpath(*parts)
    if not target.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
        raise ValueError("Unsafe path in trainer archive.")
    return target


def extract(path: str, destination: str) -> None:
    root = Path(destination)
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    total = count = 0

    def write(name, blocks, is_dir=False):
        nonlocal total, count
        count += 1
        if count > MAX_FILES:
            raise ValueError("Trainer archive contains too many files.")
        target = _target(root, name)
        if is_dir:
            target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            return
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with target.open("xb") as out:
            for block in blocks:
                total += len(block)
                if total > MAX_BYTES:
                    raise ValueError("Trainer archive is too large.")
                out.write(block)

    if zipfile.is_zipfile(path):
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as archive:
            if sum(e.file_size for e in archive.infolist()) > MAX_BYTES:
                raise ValueError("Trainer archive is too large.")
            for entry in archive.infolist():
                mode = entry.external_attr >> 16
                if stat.S_ISLNK(mode) or (stat.S_IFMT(mode) not in (0, stat.S_IFREG, stat.S_IFDIR)):
                    raise ValueError("Links and special files are not allowed in trainer archives.")
                with archive.open(entry) as source:
                    write(entry.filename, iter(lambda: source.read(256 * 1024), b""), entry.is_dir())
    else:
        try:
            import libarchive
        except (ImportError, OSError, TypeError) as e:
            raise RuntimeError("7z/RAR extraction requires libarchive on this device.") from e
        with libarchive.file_reader(path) as archive:
            for entry in archive:
                if entry.issym or entry.islnk or not (entry.isdir or entry.isfile):
                    raise ValueError("Links and special files are not allowed in trainer archives.")
                write(entry.pathname, entry.get_blocks(), entry.isdir)
    if not total:
        raise ValueError("Trainer archive has no file content.")

```

`py_modules/trainer_db.py`:

```py
"""
The local copy of GCM's trainer search database: the same JSON files GCM
downloads into its own "db" folder, kept under this plugin's settings dir
(gcm_bridge.DATABASE_PATH) so search works offline once fetched.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time

import data_source
import http_download
from gcm_bridge import DATABASE_PATH

# file name -> upstream S3 path (see upstream's threads/other_threads.py Fetch*Data)
DATABASE_FILES = {
    "fling_archive.json": "GCM/Data/fling_archive.json",
    "fling_main.json": "GCM/Data/fling_main.json",
    "xiaoxing.json": "GCM/Data/xiaoxing.json",
    "cheat_table.json": "GCM/Data/cheat_table.json",
    "gcm_trainers.json": "GCM/Data/gcm_trainers.json",
    "translations.json": "GCM/Data/translations.json",
}


def load(file_name: str):
    path = os.path.join(DATABASE_PATH, file_name)
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def status() -> dict:
    files = {}
    for name in DATABASE_FILES:
        path = os.path.join(DATABASE_PATH, name)
        files[name] = os.path.getmtime(path) if os.path.isfile(path) else None
    return {
        "configured": data_source.is_configured(),
        "data_source": data_source.status(),
        "files": files,
        "ready": all(files.values()),
    }


def refresh() -> dict:
    """Re-download every database file. Returns {success, errors: {file: message}}."""
    errors = {}
    for name, s3_path in DATABASE_FILES.items():
        try:
            url = data_source.get_signed_download_url(s3_path)
            http_download.download(url, DATABASE_PATH, filename=name)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - reported per file, others still attempted
            errors[name] = str(e)
    return {"success": not errors, "errors": errors, "finished_at": time.time()}

```

`py_modules/trainer_download.py`:

```py
"""Bounded download jobs; one atomic commit publishes each installation."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

import action_log
import data_source
import http_download
import trainer_archive
import trainer_library

_jobs: dict[str, dict] = {}
_tasks: set[asyncio.Task] = set()
_cancel: dict[str, threading.Event] = {}
_lock = threading.RLock()
_closing = False
_slots = None
ACTIVE_STATES = {"queued", "downloading", "extracting"}


class DownloadCancelled(Exception):
    pass


def resume():
    global _closing
    _closing = False


async def shutdown():
    global _closing
    with _lock:
        _closing = True
        for token in _cancel.values():
            token.set()
    # Cancelling to_thread alone does not stop its worker. Wait for cleanup.
    if _tasks:
        await asyncio.gather(*list(_tasks), return_exceptions=True)


def jobs():
    with _lock:
        return [{**job, "app_ids": list(job["app_ids"])} for job in _jobs.values()]


def cancel(job_id: str, app_id: str):
    with _lock:
        job = _jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job or app_id not in job["app_ids"]:
            raise ValueError("Unknown download for this game.")
        if job["state"] in ACTIVE_STATES:
            _cancel[job_id].set()
        return dict(job)


def start(trainer, download_dir=None, app_id="", expected_directory=""):
    global _slots
    root = os.path.realpath(download_dir or trainer_library.directory())
    if expected_directory and root != os.path.realpath(expected_directory):
        raise ValueError("library_changed")
    with _lock:
        if _closing:
            raise ValueError("plugin_stopping")
        for job in _jobs.values():
            if job["trainer_id"] == trainer["id"] and job["directory"] == root and job["state"] in ACTIVE_STATES:
                if app_id and app_id not in job["app_ids"]:
                    job["app_ids"].append(app_id)
                return job
        for key in [key for key, job in _jobs.items() if job["state"] not in ACTIVE_STATES][:-49]:
            del _jobs[key]
        job = {
            "id": uuid.uuid4().hex, "trainer_id": trainer["id"], "trainer_name": trainer["trainer_name"],
            "state": "queued", "downloaded": 0, "total": 0, "path": None, "error": None,
            "directory": root, "app_ids": [app_id] if app_id else [], "started_at": time.time(),
        }
        _jobs[job["id"]] = job
        token = _cancel[job["id"]] = threading.Event()
        if not _tasks:
            _slots = asyncio.Semaphore(2)
        task = asyncio.create_task(_run(job, dict(trainer), token))
        _tasks.add(task)
        task.add_done_callback(_tasks.discard)
        return job


def _safe_folder_name(name):
    name = name.replace(": ", " - ").replace(":", "-").replace("/", "_").replace("?", "")
    name = re.sub(r'[<>"\\|*\x00-\x1f]', "", name).strip(" .")
    return name.encode("utf-8")[:150].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore") or "trainer"


def _install(job, trainer, token):
    def check():
        if token.is_set():
            raise DownloadCancelled()

    def progress(done, total):
        check()
        with _lock:
            job.update(downloaded=done, total=total)

    check()
    root = Path(job["directory"])
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix=".gcm-download-", dir=root) as temporary:
        staging = Path(temporary)
        job["state"] = "downloading"
        url = data_source.get_signed_download_url(trainer["url"])
        check()
        downloaded = Path(http_download.download(url, str(staging / "download"), progress))
        check()
        content = staging / "content"
        content.mkdir()
        if downloaded.suffix.lower() in trainer_archive.ARCHIVE_EXTENSIONS:
            job["state"] = "extracting"
            trainer_archive.extract(str(downloaded), str(content))
        else:
            shutil.move(str(downloaded), content / downloaded.name)
        if not any(p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in trainer_library.TRAINER_EXTENSIONS for p in content.rglob("*")):
            raise ValueError("No .exe or .CT trainer files found in the download.")
        # Joining jobs, shutdown and publication share this lock. Metadata and
        # files are published together; cleanup cannot undo a committed success.
        with _lock:
            check()
            metadata = {k: trainer.get(k, "") for k in ("trainer_name", "game_name", "version", "origin")}
            metadata.update(schema_version=1, trainer_id=trainer["id"], app_ids=list(job["app_ids"]))
            trainer_library.write_json(content / trainer_library.METADATA, metadata)
            target = root / f"{_safe_folder_name(trainer['trainer_name'])} [{job['id'][:8]}]"
            content.rename(target)
            job.update(path=str(target), state="done")


async def _run(job, trainer, token):
    try:
        async with _slots:
            worker = asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(_install, job, trainer, token))
            try:
                await asyncio.shield(worker)
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                token.set()
                await worker
    except (DownloadCancelled, asyncio.CancelledError):
        if job["state"] != "done":
            job["state"] = "cancelled"
    except Exception as error:
        if job["state"] != "done":
            job.update(state="cancelled" if token.is_set() else "error", error=None if token.is_set() else str(error))
        else:
            action_log.log_warning(f"installed; temporary cleanup failed: {error}")
    finally:
        _cancel.pop(job["id"], None)
        if job["state"] == "done":
            action_log.log_event(f"installed {job['trainer_name']} -> {job['path']}")
        elif job["state"] == "error":
            action_log.log_warning(f"download failed: {job['error']}")

```

`py_modules/trainer_library.py`:

```py
"""A shared on-disk trainer library, including existing GCM folders."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import uuid
import tempfile
import threading
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path

import decky

DEFAULT_DIRECTORY = os.path.join(decky.DECKY_USER_HOME, "GCM Trainers")
SETTINGS_PATH = os.path.join(decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_SETTINGS_DIR, "library.json")
METADATA = ".gcm-trainer.json"
TRAINER_EXTENSIONS = {".exe", ".bat", ".ct"}
_lock = threading.RLock()


def write_json(path: str | Path, data: dict) -> None:
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = None
    try:
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode="w", encoding="utf-8", dir=path.parent, delete=False) as out:
            temporary = out.name
            json.dump(data, out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        if temporary and os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)


def read_json(path: str | Path) -> dict:
    try:
        value = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {}


def directory() -> str:
    with _lock:
        value = read_json(SETTINGS_PATH).get("directory")
        return value if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() else DEFAULT_DIRECTORY


def set_directory(path: str) -> str:
    if not path.strip():
        raise ValueError("Choose an existing folder.")
    target = Path(os.path.expanduser(path)).resolve(strict=True)
    if not target.is_dir():
        raise ValueError("Choose a folder, not a file.")
    # Confirm that downloads can actually be installed here before saving.
    with tempfile.TemporaryFile(dir=target):
        pass
    with _lock:
        write_json(SETTINGS_PATH, {"directory": str(target)})
    return str(target)


def _files(folder: Path) -> list[Path]:
    found = []
    for current, dirs, files in os.walk(folder, followlinks=False):
        dirs[:] = sorted(d for d in dirs if not d.startswith(".") and not (Path(current) / d).is_symlink())
        for name in sorted(files):
            path = Path(current) / name
            if not path.is_symlink() and path.suffix.lower() in TRAINER_EXTENSIONS:
                found.append(path)
    return found


def _normalized(text: str) -> str:
    return "".join(c for c in unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).casefold() if c.isalnum())


def _names(game_name: str) -> list[str]:
    names = [game_name] if game_name else []
    try:
        import gcm_bridge
        index = gcm_bridge.load_search_index()
        index.translation_index.refresh()
        for lang in ("en", "zh"):
            translated = index.translation_index.translate(game_name, lang)
            if translated:
                names.append(translated)
    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError):
        pass  # Existing files remain usable without the online database/deps.
    return list(dict.fromkeys(names))


def _matches(entry: dict, app_id: str, names: list[str]) -> bool:
    ids = entry["app_ids"]
    if ids:
        return app_id in ids
    known_name = entry["game_name"]
    if known_name:
        return _normalized(known_name) in {_normalized(n) for n in names}
    # Imported files have no metadata. Keep word/number boundaries so e.g.
    # Portal does not accidentally include Portal 2.
    candidates = [entry["name"], *entry["files"]]
    for candidate in candidates:
        candidate = re.sub(r"^\[[^\]]+\]\s*", "", Path(candidate).stem)
        for name in names:
            pattern = re.escape(name).replace(r"\ ", r"[\s_.-]+")
            if re.search(r"(?<!\w)" + pattern + r"(?!\w|[\s_.-]+\d)", candidate, re.I):
                return True
            # Chinese GCM names commonly use 《title》 without whitespace.
            if any("\u4e00" <= c <= "\u9fff" for c in name) and f"《{name}》" in candidate:
                return True
    return False


def scan(app_id: str = "", game_name: str = "") -> dict:
    root = Path(directory())
    if str(root) == DEFAULT_DIRECTORY:
        root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    entries = []
    names = _names(game_name) if app_id and game_name else []
    for child in sorted(root.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.casefold()):
        if child.name.startswith(".") or child.is_symlink():
            continue
        files = _files(child) if child.is_dir() else ([child] if child.suffix.lower() in TRAINER_EXTENSIONS else [])
        if not files:
            continue
        metadata = read_json(child / METADATA) if child.is_dir() else {}
        # Treat malformed fields as unknown; one damaged entry must not hide the library.
        metadata = {k: v for k, v in metadata.items() if isinstance(v, str) or k == "app_ids"}
        ids = metadata.get("app_ids")
        metadata["app_ids"] = [i for i in ids if isinstance(i, str) and i.isascii() and i.isdigit()] if isinstance(ids, list) else []
        entry = {
            "id": hashlib.sha256(str(child).encode()).hexdigest()[:20],
            "name": metadata.get("trainer_name") or (child.stem if child.is_file() else child.name),
            "path": str(child),
            "files": [str(p.relative_to(child if child.is_dir() else root)) for p in files],
            "trainer_id": metadata.get("trainer_id", ""),
            "game_name": metadata.get("game_name", ""),
            "app_ids": metadata.get("app_ids", []),
            "version": metadata.get("version", ""),
            "origin": metadata.get("origin", ""),
        }
        if not app_id or _matches(entry, app_id, names):
            entries.append(entry)
    return {"directory": str(root), "trainers": entries}


def entry(entry_id: str, expected_directory: str) -> dict:
    if os.path.realpath(expected_directory) != os.path.realpath(directory()):
        raise ValueError("library_changed")
    for item in scan()["trainers"]:
        if item["id"] == entry_id:
            return item
    raise ValueError("Trainer no longer exists. Refresh the library.")


def executable(entry_id: str, filename: str, expected_directory: str) -> str:
    with _lock:
        item = entry(entry_id, expected_directory)
        if filename not in item["files"]:
            raise ValueError("Unknown trainer file.")
        base = Path(item["path"])
        target = (base / filename if base.is_dir() else base).resolve(strict=True)
        root = Path(directory()).resolve(strict=True)
        if not target.is_relative_to(root) or not target.is_file() or target.suffix.lower() not in {".exe", ".bat"}:
            raise ValueError("Choose an .exe or .bat program. Cheat Tables require Cheat Engine.")
        return str(target)


def import_file(source: str, app_id: str, game_name: str, expected_directory: str) -> dict:
    with _lock:
        if os.path.realpath(expected_directory) != os.path.realpath(directory()):
            raise ValueError("library_changed")
        original = Path(source).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
        if not original.is_file() or original.suffix.lower() not in TRAINER_EXTENSIONS:
            raise ValueError("Choose an .exe, .bat or .CT file.")
        root = Path(directory()).resolve(strict=True)
        # Publish files and their game association together without replacing existing files.
        committed = False
        try:
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix=".gcm-import-", dir=root) as temporary:
                content = Path(temporary) / "content"
                content.mkdir()
                shutil.copy2(original, content / original.name)
                write_json(content / METADATA, {"schema_version": 1, "trainer_name": original.stem,
                           "game_name": game_name, "app_ids": [app_id], "origin": "Local file"})
                target = root / f"Imported [{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}]"
                content.rename(target)
                committed = True
        except OSError:
            if not committed:
                raise
            decky.logger.warning("Imported trainer; temporary cleanup failed.")
        return {"path": str(target), "filename": original.name}


def delete(entry_id: str, expected_directory: str, app_id: str) -> None:
    with _lock:
        item = entry(entry_id, expected_directory)
        if item["app_ids"] and any(value != app_id for value in item["app_ids"]):
            raise ValueError("This trainer is shared with another game and cannot be deleted here.")
        root = Path(directory()).resolve(strict=True)
        target = Path(item["path"])
        resolved = target.resolve(strict=True)
        if target.is_symlink() or resolved.parent != root:
            raise ValueError("Trainer is outside the current library.")
        # Only plugin-managed folders can be recursively deleted. Existing folders may
        # contain unrelated files and must be removed by the user outside the plugin.
        if target.is_dir():
            if read_json(target / METADATA).get("schema_version") != 1:
                raise ValueError("Only trainers downloaded or imported by this plugin can be deleted.")
            shutil.rmtree(target)
        else:
            target.unlink()

```

`py_modules/trainer_search.py`:

```py
"""Decky result IDs and isolation around the original GCM search worker."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib

import gcm_bridge
import gcm_runtime


class SearchError(Exception):
    pass


def trainer_id(trainer):
    return hashlib.sha1(f"{trainer['origin']}|{trainer['url']}".encode()).hexdigest()[:16]


def search(keyword: str, lang: str = "en") -> list[dict]:
    index = gcm_bridge.load_search_index()
    from _gcm_core.download import DownloadBaseThread
    from _gcm_core.search import DownloadDisplayThread
    keyword = keyword.strip()
    if not gcm_bridge.is_chinese(keyword) and len(index.sanitize(keyword)) < index.MIN_QUERY_LEN:
        raise SearchError("keyword_too_short")
    # GCM shares class-level results and language settings. Serialize searches,
    # including translation workers, then return detached per-request records.
    with gcm_runtime.SEARCH_LOCK:
        gcm_runtime.settings["language"] = "zh_CN" if lang == "zh" else "en_US"
        index.translation_index.refresh()
        if not index.translation_index.has_data():
            raise SearchError("database_missing")
        worker = DownloadDisplayThread(keyword)
        worker.status_pause_time = 0
        worker.run()
        results = [dict(entry) for entry in DownloadBaseThread.trainer_urls]
        failures = [event for event in worker.message.events if len(event) > 1 and event[1] == "failure"]
        if failures and not (len(failures) == 1 and failures[0][0] == "No search results found."):
            raise SearchError("database_missing" if any("update" in str(e[0]).lower() for e in failures) else "search_failed")
        for entry in results:
            entry["id"] = trainer_id(entry)
        return results

```

`py_modules/vdf_lite.py`:

```py
"""
Minimal recursive-descent parser for Valve's KeyValues (VDF) text format,
just enough to read libraryfolders.vdf, appmanifest_*.acf and SteamCMD's
app_info_print output. Not a general-purpose VDF library.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict


def _skip_whitespace_and_comments(text: str, i: int) -> int:
    n = len(text)
    while i < n:
        c = text[i]
        if c in " \t\r\n":
            i += 1
            continue
        if c == "/" and i + 1 < n and text[i + 1] == "/":
            while i < n and text[i] not in "\r\n":
                i += 1
            continue
        break
    return i


def _read_string(text: str, i: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
    n = len(text)
    if i < n and text[i] == '"':
        i += 1
        start = i
        buf = []
        while i < n and text[i] != '"':
            if text[i] == "\\" and i + 1 < n:
                buf.append(text[i + 1])
                i += 2
                continue
            buf.append(text[i])
            i += 1
        return "".join(buf), i + 1
    # Unquoted token (rare in these files, but be lenient)
    start = i
    while i < n and text[i] not in ' \t\r\n{}"':
        i += 1
    return text[start:i], i


def parse(text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Parses a VDF document and returns {rootKey: {...}} (or {} on failure)."""
    i = 0
    n = len(text)
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {}

    def parse_object(i: int) -> tuple[Dict[str, Any], int]:
        obj: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        while True:
            i = _skip_whitespace_and_comments(text, i)
            if i >= n:
                break
            if text[i] == "}":
                return obj, i + 1
            key, i = _read_string(text, i)
            i = _skip_whitespace_and_comments(text, i)
            if i >= n:
                break
            if text[i] == "{":
                value, i = parse_object(i + 1)
            else:
                value, i = _read_string(text, i)
            obj[key] = value
        return obj, i

    i = _skip_whitespace_and_comments(text, i)
    while i < n:
        key, i = _read_string(text, i)
        i = _skip_whitespace_and_comments(text, i)
        if i < n and text[i] == "{":
            value, i = parse_object(i + 1)
        else:
            value, i = _read_string(text, i)
        result[key] = value
        i = _skip_whitespace_and_comments(text, i)
    return result


def get_child(obj: Any, *path: str) -> Any:
    """Case-insensitive nested lookup, mirroring ValveDataFile.GetChild usage in CreamInstaller."""
    cur = obj
    for key in path:
        if not isinstance(cur, dict):
            return None
        match = next((v for k, v in cur.items() if k.lower() == key.lower()), None)
        cur = match
    return cur

```

`requirements.txt`:

```txt
# Runtime deps of gcm/src/scripts/search_index.py (pypinyin, rapidfuzz, zhon) and
# of an upstream-style secret_config.signed_get (requests), vendored into
# py_modules/_vendor/ by scripts/vendor_deps.sh (Decky's bundled Python has no pip).
pypinyin
rapidfuzz
requests
zhon

# Pure-Python bridge to SteamOS libarchive (7z/RAR extraction).
libarchive-c>=5.3,<6

# Upstream Retry uses backoff_jitter (urllib3 2+).
urllib3>=2,<3
