`scripts/DEV.md`:

```md
# VM live development

Install the plugin ZIP in Decky once, enable SSH in the VM, and configure SSH key
login from the host. The guest needs `bash`, `rsync`, `sudo`, `flock`, and Decky.
On Windows, run `pnpm dev` using Git Bash (installed with Git) on PATH.

Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and fill in your own SSH destination. `.env` is
Git-ignored and is never synchronized to the VM or included in plugin packages.
The blank template contains no personal hostnames, ports or usernames.

- `DECKY_HOST`: SSH host alias or `user@host`.
- `DECKY_PORT`: SSH port (defaults to the standard SSH port).
- `DECKY_SSH_IDENTITY`: optional SSH private key path.
- `DECKY_SSH_KNOWN_HOSTS`: optional pinned known-hosts file.
- `PYTHON`: optional host Python executable; Python needs pip.
- `DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION`: optional override for guest interpreter detection.

Relative paths are resolved from the repository root; `~/` is supported. Process
environment variables override values in `.env`. Do not store SSH/sudo passwords.
SSH uses a key; sudo may prompt once at startup. Requires Node 20.12 or later.

Run:

```bash
pnpm dev
```

The command checks SSH and Decky, builds the GCM core and frontend, prepares Linux
wheels for the guest's Python, and uploads the source through SSH. A remote TTY
session backs up the installed plugin, creates symlinks to a guest source mirror,
enables `LIVE_RELOAD`, and restarts Decky.

Local edits are debounced for one second, rebuilt and synchronized automatically.
Deleted source files are removed from the mirror. Unchanged files retain their
timestamps. Decky's recursive watcher does not follow the development symlinks,
so after applying an upload the session touches the `main.py` symlink itself
to request Decky's native plugin reload. This also handles rsync's atomic renames.
Python or dependency changes restart Decky; frontend changes use the native plugin
reload, which reloads both the plugin backend and frontend.
Startup and backend restarts wait for Decky's ten-second watcher startup delay
before requesting a reload, so Steam also receives the initial development build.
An already-mounted Steam page can require leaving and reopening the page.

After changing `scripts/dev-session.sh`, restart `pnpm dev` to load the updated
remote session script. Ordinary source edits do not require restarting it.

Ctrl+C or SSH disconnection restores the installed plugin and removes this
session's systemd override. A host heartbeat also ends abandoned sessions. Only
one development session can own the VM plugin at a time. Source and dependency
mirrors use the guest's `/tmp`; each startup repopulates them after reboot.

```

`scripts/build_gcm_core.py`:

```py
"""Generate Qt-free modules from the pinned GCM submodule. Never edit gcm/."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import ast
import builtins
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import os
import symtable
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SCRIPTS = REPO / "gcm" / "src" / "scripts"
MODULES = REPO / "py_modules"
sys.path.insert(0, str(MODULES))


def parse(name):
    path = SCRIPTS / name
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ValueError("GCM submodule missing; run git submodule update --init")
    return ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))


def functions(tree, names):
    selected = [copy.deepcopy(n) for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in names]
    if {n.name for n in selected} != set(names):
        raise ValueError("Pinned GCM function interface changed")
    return selected


class Imports(ast.NodeTransformer):
    def visit_ImportFrom(self, node):
        if node.module in {"config", "search_index"}:
            node.level = 1
        return node


class AuthNames(ast.NodeTransformer):
    def visit_Name(self, node):
        if node.id in {"SIGNED_URL_DOWNLOAD_ENDPOINT", "CLIENT_API_KEY", "signed_get"}:
            return ast.copy_location(ast.Attribute(value=ast.Name(id="auth", ctx=ast.Load()), attr=node.id, ctx=node.ctx), node)
        return node


def worker(tree, class_name, methods, bases, attributes=()):
    original = next((n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef) and n.name == class_name), None)
    if original is None:
        raise ValueError("Pinned GCM worker class changed")
    body = functions(ast.Module(body=original.body, type_ignores=[]), methods)
    for node in original.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id in attributes for t in node.targets):
            body.insert(0, copy.deepcopy(node))
    result = ast.ClassDef(name=class_name, bases=[ast.Name(id=b, ctx=ast.Load()) for b in bases], keywords=[], body=body, decorator_list=[])
    return ast.fix_missing_locations(AuthNames().visit(result))


def validate(source, name):
    compile(source, name, "exec")
    table = symtable.symtable(source, name, "exec")
    known = {s.get_name() for s in table.get_symbols() if s.is_assigned() or s.is_imported()} | set(dir(builtins)) | {"__name__", "__file__"}
    def check(scope):
        for symbol in scope.get_symbols():
            if symbol.is_referenced() and symbol.is_global() and symbol.get_name() not in known:
                raise ValueError(f"Unadapted upstream dependency in {name}: {symbol.get_name()}")
        for child in scope.get_children():
            check(child)
    check(table)


def emit(output, name, preamble, nodes):
    source = "# Generated from the pinned GCM submodule; GPL-3.0. Do not edit.\n" + preamble + "\n" + ast.unparse(ast.fix_missing_locations(ast.Module(body=nodes, type_ignores=[]))) + "\n"
    validate(source, name)
    (output / name).write_text(source, encoding="utf-8")


def build(output, secret=None):
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    config = parse("config.py")
    names = {"APP_VERSION", "API_TIMEOUT", "RETRY_STATUSES"}
    constants = [n for n in config.body if isinstance(n, ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id in names for t in n.targets)]
    if len(constants) != len(names):
        raise ValueError("Pinned GCM configuration interface changed")
    emit(output, "config.py", "import re, unicodedata\nfrom pypinyin import lazy_pinyin\nfrom gcm_runtime import DATABASE_PATH, is_chinese", constants + functions(config, {"trainer_sort_key"}))
    index = Imports().visit(parse("search_index.py"))
    emit(output, "search_index.py", "", index.body)
    download_tree = parse("threads/download_base_thread.py")
    download_constants = [n for n in download_tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id in {"_PARALLEL_THRESHOLD", "_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT"} for t in n.targets)]
    if len(download_constants) != 2:
        raise ValueError("Pinned GCM download constants changed")
    base = worker(download_tree, "DownloadBaseThread", {
        "__init__", "request_download", "download_queued", "find_download_fname",
        "get_signed_download_url", "find_best_trainer_match", "translate_trainer", "load_json_content",
    }, ["QThread"], {"trainer_urls", "headers"})
    emit(output, "download.py", '''import hashlib, json, os, queue, threading, time, uuid
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from urllib.parse import urlparse, unquote, unquote_plus
import gcm_transport as requests
from requests.adapters import HTTPAdapter
from urllib3.util.retry import Retry
from gcm_runtime import BackendWorker as QThread, DATABASE_PATH, settings, is_chinese, tr, auth, quiet as print
from .config import API_TIMEOUT, RETRY_STATUSES
from .search_index import translation_index
''', download_constants + functions(download_tree, {"create_http_session"}) + [base])
    search_tree = parse("threads/download_display_thread.py")
    search_class = next(n for n in search_tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef) and n.name == "DownloadDisplayThread")
    methods = {n.name for n in search_class.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef)}
    expected = {"__init__", "run", "translate_keyword", "keyword_match", "search_from_fling_archive", "search_from_fling_main", "search_from_xiaoxing", "search_from_ct", "search_from_gcm"}
    if methods != expected:
        raise ValueError("Pinned GCM search worker interface changed")
    emit(output, "search.py", '''import concurrent.futures, time, traceback
from gcm_runtime import settings, is_chinese, tr, quiet as print
from .config import trainer_sort_key
from .search_index import MIN_QUERY_LEN, QueryMatcher, sanitize, translation_index
from .download import DownloadBaseThread
''', [worker(search_tree, "DownloadDisplayThread", expected, ["DownloadBaseThread"])])
    if secret:
        from gcm_auth_adapter import render
        source = render(Path(secret).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        validate(source, "auth.py")
        path = output / "auth.py"
        path.write_text(source, encoding="utf-8")
        os.chmod(path, 0o600)
    else:
        # A no-secret build must not accidentally reuse a previous release's key.
        (output / "auth.py").unlink(missing_ok=True)
    (output / "__init__.py").write_text('"""Generated GCM core (GPL-3.0)."""\n', encoding="utf-8")
    files = ("config.py", "search_index.py", "threads/download_base_thread.py", "threads/download_display_thread.py")
    manifest = {name: hashlib.sha256((SCRIPTS / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for name in files}
    (output / "sources.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print("Generated GCM core; authentication " + ("included" if secret else "not configured"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--secret", default=os.environ.get("GCM_SECRET_CONFIG_PATH"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=MODULES / "_gcm_core")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    secret = args.secret
    if not secret and (MODULES / "secret_config.py").is_file():
        secret = MODULES / "secret_config.py"
    try:
        build(args.output, secret)
    except Exception as error:
        # SyntaxError can include source lines from the private input. Never print it.
        print(f"GCM core generation failed ({type(error).__name__}); check source compatibility and configuration.", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

```

`scripts/dev-session.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# One privileged session owns setup and cleanup, including after its SSH TTY closes.
set -euo pipefail
if (( EUID != 0 )); then exec sudo -- bash "$0"; fi
GUEST_UID="${SUDO_UID:?Start this script as the SSH user}"
GUEST_HOME="$(getent passwd "$GUEST_UID" | cut -d: -f6)"
DEV="/tmp/decky-gcm-dev-$GUEST_UID"
PLUGIN="$GUEST_HOME/homebrew/plugins/GameCheatsManager"
OVERRIDE="/etc/systemd/system/plugin_loader.service.d/90-gcm-remote-dev.conf"
ENTRIES=(dist py_modules main.py package.json plugin.json)
test ! -L "$DEV" && [[ $(stat -c %u "$DEV") == "$GUEST_UID" ]]
exec 9>"$DEV/session.lock"
flock -n 9 || { echo '[dev] Another development session is running.' >&2; exit 1; }
test -d "$PLUGIN" || { echo '[dev] Install the ZIP first.' >&2; exit 1; }
linked=0
cleanup() {
    trap - EXIT HUP INT TERM
    set +e
    echo '[dev] Restoring the installed plugin…'
    if (( linked )); then
        systemctl stop plugin_loader
        for name in "${ENTRIES[@]}"; do
            target="$PLUGIN/$name"
            backup="$target.dev-backup"
            if [[ -L "$target" && $(readlink "$target") == "$DEV/current/$name" ]]; then
                rm -- "$target"
                if [[ -e "$backup" || -L "$backup" ]]; then mv -- "$backup" "$target"; fi
            elif [[ ! -e "$target" && ! -L "$target" && ( -e "$backup" || -L "$backup" ) ]]; then
                mv -- "$backup" "$target"
            fi
        done
        rm -f -- "$OVERRIDE"
        systemctl daemon-reload
        systemctl start plugin_loader
    fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 0' HUP INT TERM

backend_signature() {
    if [[ -d "$DEV/current/py_modules" ]]; then
        find "$DEV/current/py_modules" "$DEV/current/main.py" "$DEV/current/package.json" "$DEV/current/plugin.json" -type f ! -name '*.pyc' ! -path '*/__pycache__/*' -print0 | sort -z | xargs -0 sha256sum | sha256sum
    fi
}

reload_after_start() {
    # Decky v3 waits ten seconds before enabling its watcher. A service
    # restart alone can leave Steam running the previous frontend module.
    sleep 11
    touch -h -- "$PLUGIN/main.py"
}

apply_pending() {
    [[ -f "$DEV/pending" ]] || return 0
    mv -- "$DEV/pending" "$DEV/processing"
    IFS= read -r incoming < "$DEV/processing"
    [[ "$incoming" == "$DEV"/.upload.* && -d "$incoming" && ! -L "$incoming" ]] || { echo '[dev] Invalid upload path.' >&2; exit 1; }
    mkdir -p "$DEV/current"
    previous=$(backend_signature)
    # Keep the mirror stable. Vendor is omitted on ordinary edits, so retain
    # it while removing deleted source files.
    rsync -rc --delete --exclude='/py_modules/_vendor/' "$incoming/" "$DEV/current/"
    if [[ -d "$incoming/py_modules/_vendor" ]]; then
        mkdir -p "$DEV/current/py_modules/_vendor"
        rsync -rc --delete "$incoming/py_modules/_vendor/" "$DEV/current/py_modules/_vendor/"
    fi
    current=$(backend_signature)
    rm -rf -- "$incoming"
    rm -f -- "$DEV/processing"
    if (( linked )) && [[ "$previous" != "$current" ]]; then
        echo '[dev] Backend changed; restarting Decky…'
        systemctl restart plugin_loader
        reload_after_start
    elif (( linked )); then
        # Decky's recursive watcher does not follow our dist symlink, and
        # rsync publishes files by rename. Notify it through the main.py link
        # in the watched plugin directory, after the whole upload is applied.
        # -h changes the link itself rather than its unwatched /tmp target.
        touch -h -- "$PLUGIN/main.py"
        echo '[dev] Frontend synchronized; Decky live reload requested.'
    fi
}

apply_pending
[[ -f "$DEV/current/main.py" ]] || { echo '[dev] Initial source upload is missing.' >&2; exit 1; }
systemctl stop plugin_loader
linked=1
for name in "${ENTRIES[@]}"; do
    target="$PLUGIN/$name"
    backup="$target.dev-backup"
    if [[ -L "$target" && $(readlink "$target") == "$DEV/current/$name" ]]; then continue; fi
    [[ ! -e "$backup" && ! -L "$backup" ]] || { echo "[dev] Existing backup requires recovery: $backup" >&2; exit 1; }
    if [[ -e "$target" || -L "$target" ]]; then mv -- "$target" "$backup"; fi
    ln -s -- "$DEV/current/$name" "$target"
done
mkdir -p -- "$(dirname "$OVERRIDE")"
printf '[Service]\nEnvironment=LIVE_RELOAD=1\n' | tee "$OVERRIDE" >/dev/null
systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl start plugin_loader
reload_after_start
touch "$DEV/heartbeat"
chown "$GUEST_UID" "$DEV/heartbeat"
echo '[dev] Live session ready. Source changes sync automatically; Ctrl+C restores the installed version.'
while sleep 1; do
    if (( $(date +%s) - $(stat -c %Y "$DEV/heartbeat") > 60 )); then
        echo '[dev] Host heartbeat expired.'
        exit 1
    fi
    apply_pending
done

```

`scripts/dev.mjs`:

```mjs
import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { parseEnv } from "node:util";
import { homedir } from "node:os";

const repo = fileURLToPath(new URL("../", import.meta.url));
const cache = path.join(repo, ".decky-dev");
const guest = '/tmp/decky-gcm-dev-$(id -u)';
const log = (message) => console.log(`[dev] ${message}`);

function run(command, args, options = {}) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(command, args, { cwd: repo, stdio: "inherit", ...options });
    child.once("error", reject);
    child.once("exit", (code) => code === 0 ? resolve() : reject(new Error(`${path.basename(command)} exited with ${code}`)));
  });
}

function executable(name) {
  const git = process.platform === "win32" ? [
    path.join(process.env.ProgramFiles || "C:/Program Files", "Git/usr/bin", `${name}.exe`),
    path.join(process.env.USERPROFILE || "", "scoop/apps/git/current/usr/bin", `${name}.exe`),
  ].find((candidate) => fs.existsSync(candidate)) : null;
  return git || name;
}

function fingerprint() {
  const hash = createHash("sha256");
  function visit(target) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(target)) return;
    const stat = fs.lstatSync(target);
    if (stat.isSymbolicLink()) return;
    if (stat.isDirectory()) {
      if (["_vendor", "_gcm_core", "__pycache__"].includes(path.basename(target))) return;
      for (const name of fs.readdirSync(target).sort()) visit(path.join(target, name));
    } else hash.update(`${path.relative(repo, target)}:${stat.size}:${stat.mtimeMs}\n`);
  }
  for (const name of ["src", "main.py", "py_modules", "requirements.txt", "package.json", "rollup.config.js", "scripts/build_gcm_core.py", "gcm/src/scripts", "scripts/dev-session.sh"]) visit(path.join(repo, name));
  return hash.digest("hex");
}

// Only runtime code is synchronized. Raw secrets, Windows wheels and cache files
// stay on the host; the generated signer is sent through encrypted SSH.
export function copyRuntime(source, destination, vendor) {
  fs.mkdirSync(destination, { recursive: true });
  for (const name of ["dist", "main.py", "package.json", "plugin.json", "py_modules"]) {
    fs.cpSync(path.join(source, name), path.join(destination, name), {
      recursive: true, preserveTimestamps: true,
      filter: (file) => {
        const relative = path.relative(source, file).split(path.sep);
        return !fs.lstatSync(file).isSymbolicLink() && !relative.some((part) => ["_vendor", "__pycache__", "secret_config.py"].includes(part)) && !file.endsWith(".pyc");
      },
    });
  }
  if (vendor) fs.cpSync(vendor, path.join(destination, "py_modules/_vendor"), { recursive: true });
}

export function loadDevEnv(directory, environment = process.env) {
  const filename = path.join(directory, ".env");
  const local = fs.existsSync(filename) ? parseEnv(fs.readFileSync(filename, "utf8")) : {};
  return { ...local, ...environment };
}

const localPath = (value) => path.resolve(repo, value.startsWith("~/") ? path.join(homedir(), value.slice(2)) : value);

async function main() {
  const env = loadDevEnv(repo);
  const host = env.DECKY_HOST;
  const port = Number(env.DECKY_PORT || 22);
  if (!host || !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.@:[\]-]+$/.test(host) || !Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
    throw new Error('Set DECKY_HOST and DECKY_PORT in .env (SSH key login required).');
  }
  const python = env.PYTHON || (process.platform === "win32" ? "python" : "python3");
  const ssh = executable("ssh");
  const tar = executable("tar");
  const sshArgs = ["-p", String(port), "-o", "BatchMode=yes", "-o", "ConnectTimeout=8", "-o", "ServerAliveInterval=10", "-o", "ServerAliveCountMax=3"];
  if (env.DECKY_SSH_IDENTITY) sshArgs.push("-i", localPath(env.DECKY_SSH_IDENTITY));
  if (env.DECKY_SSH_KNOWN_HOSTS) sshArgs.push("-o", `UserKnownHostsFile=${localPath(env.DECKY_SSH_KNOWN_HOSTS)}`);
  const remote = (command, options = {}) => run(ssh, [...sshArgs, host, command], options);
  fs.mkdirSync(cache, { recursive: true });
  const probe = spawnSync(ssh, [...sshArgs, host, `set -e; test -d "$HOME/homebrew/plugins/GameCheatsManager" || { echo 'Install the plugin ZIP first.' >&2; exit 1; }; command -v rsync >/dev/null || { echo 'rsync is required in the VM.' >&2; exit 1; }; grep -aoE 'libpython3\\.[0-9]+' "$HOME/homebrew/services/PluginLoader" | sort -u`], { encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "inherit"] });
  const version = env.DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION || probe.stdout?.match(/libpython(3\.\d+)/)?.[1];
  if (probe.status !== 0 || !/^3\.\d+$/.test(version || "")) throw new Error("Cannot inspect the VM. Check SSH, installed Decky, rsync and DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION.");
  log(`SSH connected; Decky Python ${version}.`);

  let vendorKey = "";
  let stopping = false;
  let session;
  let busy = false;
  const stop = () => {
    if (stopping) return;
    stopping = true;
    // The remote foreground session owns cleanup and sudo's TTY ticket.
    session?.kill("SIGTERM");
  };
  process.once("SIGINT", stop);
  process.once("SIGTERM", stop);

  async function synchronize() {
    log("Building GCM core and frontend…");
    await run(process.execPath, ["node_modules/rollup/dist/bin/rollup", "-c"], { env: { ...env, PYTHON: python } });
    if (stopping) return;
    const key = createHash("sha256").update(version).update(fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo, "requirements.txt"))).digest("hex").slice(0, 16);
    const vendor = path.join(cache, `vendor-${key}`);
    const dependenciesChanged = vendorKey !== key;
    if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(vendor, ".ready"))) {
      log("Preparing Linux dependencies for the VM…");
      await run(python, ["-m", "pip", "install", "--target", vendor, "--python-version", version, "--implementation", "cp", "--abi", `cp${version.replace(".", "")}`, "--platform", "manylinux_2_28_x86_64", "--platform", "manylinux2014_x86_64", "--only-binary=:all:", "--no-compile", "-r", "requirements.txt"]);
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(vendor, ".ready"), "");
    }
    if (stopping) return;
    const stage = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(cache, "upload-"));
    try {
      copyRuntime(repo, stage, dependenciesChanged ? vendor : null);
      // This metadata lets the receiver keep the frontend's watched directory
      // stable and restart the backend only if Python/dependencies changed.
      const receive = `set -eu; dev="${guest}"; test ! -L "$dev"; if test -e "$dev"; then test "$(stat -c %u "$dev")" = "$(id -u)"; fi; mkdir -p -m 700 "$dev"; incoming=$(mktemp -d "$dev/.upload.XXXXXX"); tar -xf - -C "$incoming"; printf '%s\\n' "$incoming" > "$dev/pending.tmp"; mv "$dev/pending.tmp" "$dev/pending"`;
      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const sender = spawn(tar, ["-cf", "-", "-C", stage, "."], { stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "inherit"] });
        const receiver = spawn(ssh, [...sshArgs, host, receive], { stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"] });
        sender.stdout.pipe(receiver.stdin);
        let remaining = 2;
        for (const child of [sender, receiver]) {
          child.once("error", reject);
          child.once("exit", (code) => code !== 0 ? reject(new Error("Source synchronization failed")) : --remaining === 0 && resolve());
        }
        receiver.stdin.on("error", () => {});
      });
      vendorKey = key;
      log("Source synchronized.");
    } finally {
      const resolved = fs.realpathSync(stage);
      if (path.dirname(resolved) !== fs.realpathSync(cache)) throw new Error("Invalid staging path");
      fs.rmSync(resolved, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  }

  await synchronize();
  // Upload the session script without putting its contents in a shell command.
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(ssh, [...sshArgs, host, `cat > "${guest}/session.sh"`], { stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"] });
    child.once("error", reject);
    child.once("exit", (code) => code === 0 ? resolve() : reject(new Error("Could not upload development session")));
    child.stdin.end(fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo, "scripts/dev-session.sh"), "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n"));
  });
  log("Starting live session; enter the VM's sudo password if prompted. Ctrl+C restores the installed plugin.");
  session = spawn(ssh, [...sshArgs, "-tt", host, `bash "${guest}/session.sh"`], { stdio: "inherit" });
  const heartbeat = setInterval(() => {
    if (!stopping) void remote(`touch "${guest}/heartbeat"`).catch(() => {});
  }, 10000);
  const ended = new Promise((resolve, reject) => { session.once("error", reject); session.once("exit", (code) => { stopping = true; resolve(code); }); });
  let settled = fingerprint();
  let pending = settled;
  let changedAt = 0;
  const timer = setInterval(async () => {
    if (busy || stopping) return;
    busy = true;
    try {
      const current = fingerprint();
      if (current !== pending) { pending = current; changedAt = Date.now(); }
      if (current !== settled && Date.now() - changedAt >= 1000) {
        await synchronize();
        settled = current;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[dev] ${error.message}; waiting for the next change/retry.`);
    } finally { busy = false; }
  }, 500);
  try { await ended; } finally { clearInterval(timer); clearInterval(heartbeat); stop(); }
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  main().catch((error) => { console.error(`[dev] ${error.message}`); process.exitCode = 1; });
}

```

`scripts/vendor_deps.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Vendors requirements.txt into py_modules/_vendor/ for Decky Loader's own
# bundled Python (which has no pip and can't see system site-packages).
# DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION must match the Python inside ~/homebrew/services/PluginLoader
# (`strings PluginLoader | grep -o 'libpython3\.[0-9]*'`), since rapidfuzz ships
# compiled extensions per CPython version.
set -euo pipefail

REPO_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
TARGET="${REPO_DIR}/py_modules/_vendor"
DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION="${DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION:-3.14}"

rm -rf "$TARGET"
if command -v uv >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    uv pip install \
        --quiet \
        --target "$TARGET" \
        --python-version "$DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION" \
        --python-platform x86_64-manylinux_2_28 \
        --only-binary :all: \
        -r "${REPO_DIR}/requirements.txt"
else
    python3 -m pip install \
        --quiet \
        --target "$TARGET" \
        --python-version "$DECKY_PYTHON_VERSION" \
        --platform manylinux2014_x86_64 \
        --platform manylinux_2_17_x86_64 \
        --only-binary=:all: \
        --no-compile \
        -r "${REPO_DIR}/requirements.txt"
fi
rm -rf "$TARGET/bin"
echo "vendored into $TARGET"

```

`src/domain/options.ts`:

```ts
// Adapted from CheatDeck (https://github.com/SheffeyG/CheatDeck), GPL-3.0.
import {
  type ParseDiagnostic,
  type ParsedLaunchOptions,
  type ParsedPrefix,
  parseLaunchOptions,
  parseLiteralWord,
  parseRawWords,
  type SourceSpan,
} from "./parser";

export type LaunchOptionDefinition =
  | { kind: "environment"; name: string; value: string }
  | { kind: "prefix"; command: string; argv: readonly string[] }
  | { kind: "argument"; flag: string; argv: readonly string[] };

export type LaunchOptionsEditResult =
  | { ok: true; value: LaunchOptions; changed: boolean }
  | { ok: false; value: LaunchOptions; error: "document-not-editable" | "invalid-definition" | "invalid-result" };

interface TextEdit {
  span: SourceSpan;
  replacement: string;
}

type LaunchOptionsEdit =
  | { kind: "enable"; definition: LaunchOptionDefinition }
  | { kind: "disable"; definition: LaunchOptionDefinition };

const unquotedWord = /^[A-Za-z0-9_.,:@%+/-]+$/;
const environmentName = /^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$/;
const reservedWords = new Set(["%command%", "--"]);

const quoteWord = (value: string): string => `'${value.split("'").join(`'"'"'`)}'`;
const renderWord = (value: string): string =>
  unquotedWord.test(value) && !reservedWords.has(value) ? value : quoteWord(value);

const areValidRawWords = (words: readonly string[]): boolean => {
  if (words.length === 0) return true;
  const parsed = parseRawWords(words.join(" "));
  return parsed !== undefined && parsed.length === words.length && parsed.every((word, index) => word === words[index]);
};

const isValidFlag = (flag: string): boolean =>
  flag.startsWith("-") && flag !== "-" && flag !== "--" && unquotedWord.test(flag);

export const isValidLaunchOption = (definition: LaunchOptionDefinition): boolean => {
  switch (definition.kind) {
    case "environment":
      return environmentName.test(definition.name);
    case "prefix": {
      const words = [definition.command, ...definition.argv];
      return (
        parseLiteralWord(definition.command) !== "" &&
        !definition.command.startsWith("-") &&
        !definition.command.includes("=") &&
        areValidRawWords(words) &&
        words.every((word) => !reservedWords.has(word))
      );
    }
    case "argument":
      return (
        isValidFlag(definition.flag) &&
        areValidRawWords(definition.argv) &&
        definition.argv.every((word) => !word.startsWith("-") && word !== "%command%")
      );
  }
};

export const renderLaunchOptionDefinition = (definition: LaunchOptionDefinition): string => {
  switch (definition.kind) {
    case "environment":
      return `${definition.name}=${renderWord(definition.value)}`;
    case "prefix":
      return [definition.command, ...definition.argv].join(" ");
    case "argument":
      return [definition.flag, ...definition.argv].join(" ");
  }
};

export const parseLaunchOptionDefinition = (source: string): LaunchOptionDefinition | undefined => {
  const raw = source.trim();
  const words = parseRawWords(raw);
  if (!words) return undefined;

  const parsed = parseLaunchOptions(`${raw} %command%`);
  if (parsed.diagnostics.length > 0) return undefined;
  if (parsed.assignments.length > 0) {
    if (parsed.assignments.length !== 1 || parsed.prefixes.length > 0) return undefined;
    const { name, value } = parsed.assignments[0];
    if (value === undefined) return undefined;
    const definition = { kind: "environment" as const, name, value };
    return isValidLaunchOption(definition) ? definition : undefined;
  }

  const [first, ...argv] = words;
  if (first.includes("=")) return undefined;
  const definition: LaunchOptionDefinition = first.startsWith("-")
    ? { kind: "argument", flag: first, argv }
    : { kind: "prefix", command: first, argv };
  return isValidLaunchOption(definition) ? definition : undefined;
};

const prefixCommandMatches = (prefix: ParsedPrefix, command: string): boolean => prefix.words[0]?.raw === command;

const findWordSequenceSpans = (
  words: readonly { raw: string; span: SourceSpan }[],
  sequence: readonly string[],
  start = 0,
): SourceSpan[] => {
  if (sequence.length === 0) return [];
  const spans: SourceSpan[] = [];
  for (let index = start; index <= words.length - sequence.length; ) {
    if (!sequence.every((expected, offset) => words[index + offset].raw === expected)) {
      index++;
      continue;
    }
    spans.push({ start: words[index].span.start, end: words[index + sequence.length - 1].span.end });
    index += sequence.length;
  }
  return spans;
};

const findDefinitionSpans = (parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions, definition: LaunchOptionDefinition): SourceSpan[] => {
  switch (definition.kind) {
    case "environment":
      return parsed.assignments
        .filter((assignment) => assignment.name === definition.name && assignment.value === definition.value)
        .map((assignment) => assignment.span);
    case "prefix":
      return parsed.prefixes.flatMap((prefix) => {
        if (!prefixCommandMatches(prefix, definition.command)) return [];
        return definition.argv.length === 0
          ? [prefix.words[0].span]
          : findWordSequenceSpans(prefix.words, definition.argv, 1);
      });
    case "argument":
      return findWordSequenceSpans(parsed.arguments, [definition.flag, ...definition.argv]);
  }
};

const leadingOwnedSpan = (source: string, target: SourceSpan): SourceSpan => {
  let start = target.start;
  while (start > 0 && /[\t ]/.test(source[start - 1])) start--;
  return { start, end: target.end };
};

const prefixDeletionSpan = (parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions, index: number): SourceSpan => {
  const prefix = parsed.prefixes[index];
  if (prefix.separatorBefore) {
    return leadingOwnedSpan(parsed.source, { start: prefix.separatorBefore.start, end: prefix.span.end });
  }
  const next = parsed.prefixes[index + 1];
  if (next?.separatorBefore) return { start: prefix.span.start, end: next.separatorBefore.end };
  if (prefix.separatorAfter)
    return leadingOwnedSpan(parsed.source, { start: prefix.span.start, end: prefix.separatorAfter.end });
  return leadingOwnedSpan(parsed.source, prefix.span);
};

const mergeDeletions = (edits: readonly TextEdit[]): TextEdit[] => {
  const sorted = [...edits].sort((left, right) => left.span.start - right.span.start || left.span.end - right.span.end);
  const merged: TextEdit[] = [];
  for (const edit of sorted) {
    const previous = merged[merged.length - 1];
    if (previous && previous.replacement === "" && edit.replacement === "" && edit.span.start <= previous.span.end) {
      previous.span.end = Math.max(previous.span.end, edit.span.end);
    } else merged.push({ span: { ...edit.span }, replacement: edit.replacement });
  }
  return merged;
};

const applyTextEdits = (source: string, edits: readonly TextEdit[]): string =>
  mergeDeletions(edits)
    .sort((left, right) => right.span.start - left.span.start || right.span.end - left.span.end)
    .reduce(
      (current, edit) => current.slice(0, edit.span.start) + edit.replacement + current.slice(edit.span.end),
      source,
    );

const argumentSlotEdits = (
  parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions,
  definition: Extract<LaunchOptionDefinition, { kind: "argument" }>,
): TextEdit[] => {
  const edits: TextEdit[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < parsed.arguments.length; index++) {
    const word = parsed.arguments[index];
    if (word.raw !== definition.flag) continue;
    let end = word.span.end;
    let consumed = 0;
    while (consumed < definition.argv.length) {
      const next = parsed.arguments[index + consumed + 1];
      if (!next || next.raw.startsWith("-")) break;
      end = next.span.end;
      consumed++;
    }
    edits.push({ span: leadingOwnedSpan(parsed.source, { start: word.span.start, end }), replacement: "" });
    index += consumed;
  }
  return edits;
};

const environmentDeletionEdits = (parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions, name: string): TextEdit[] =>
  parsed.assignments
    .filter((assignment) => assignment.name === name)
    .map((assignment) => ({ span: leadingOwnedSpan(parsed.source, assignment.span), replacement: "" }));

const prefixDefinitionDeletionEdits = (
  parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions,
  definition: Extract<LaunchOptionDefinition, { kind: "prefix" }>,
): TextEdit[] => {
  const edits: TextEdit[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < parsed.prefixes.length; index++) {
    const prefix = parsed.prefixes[index];
    if (!prefixCommandMatches(prefix, definition.command)) continue;
    if (definition.argv.length === 0) {
      edits.push({ span: prefixDeletionSpan(parsed, index), replacement: "" });
      continue;
    }
    const matches = findWordSequenceSpans(prefix.words, definition.argv, 1);
    if (matches.length === 0) continue;
    const remainingWords = prefix.words.length - matches.length * definition.argv.length;
    if (remainingWords === 1) edits.push({ span: prefixDeletionSpan(parsed, index), replacement: "" });
    else {
      edits.push(...matches.map((span) => ({ span: leadingOwnedSpan(parsed.source, span), replacement: "" })));
    }
  }
  return edits;
};

const removeDefinitionEdits = (parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions, definition: LaunchOptionDefinition): TextEdit[] => {
  switch (definition.kind) {
    case "environment":
      return environmentDeletionEdits(parsed, definition.name);
    case "prefix":
      return prefixDefinitionDeletionEdits(parsed, definition);
    case "argument":
      return findDefinitionSpans(parsed, definition).map((span) => ({
        span: leadingOwnedSpan(parsed.source, span),
        replacement: "",
      }));
  }
};

const replacementDeletionEdits = (parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions, definition: LaunchOptionDefinition): TextEdit[] => {
  switch (definition.kind) {
    case "environment":
      return environmentDeletionEdits(parsed, definition.name);
    case "argument":
      return argumentSlotEdits(parsed, definition);
    case "prefix":
      return [];
  }
};

const insertionEdit = (parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions, definition: LaunchOptionDefinition): TextEdit => {
  const rendered = renderLaunchOptionDefinition(definition);
  if (parsed.implicitMarker) {
    const replacement = definition.kind === "argument" ? `%command% ${rendered}` : `${rendered} %command%`;
    return { span: { start: 0, end: parsed.source.length }, replacement };
  }
  if (!parsed.marker) throw new Error("editable launch options must have a marker");
  if (definition.kind === "environment") {
    const position = parsed.prefixes[0]?.span.start ?? parsed.marker.span.start;
    return { span: { start: position, end: position }, replacement: `${rendered} ` };
  }
  if (definition.kind === "prefix") {
    const existing = parsed.prefixes.find((prefix) => prefixCommandMatches(prefix, definition.command));
    if (existing && definition.argv.length > 0) {
      return {
        span: { start: existing.span.end, end: existing.span.end },
        replacement: ` ${definition.argv.join(" ")}`,
      };
    }
    if (parsed.prefixes.length > 0) {
      const position = parsed.prefixes[0].span.start;
      return { span: { start: position, end: position }, replacement: `${rendered} -- ` };
    }
    return {
      span: { start: parsed.marker.span.start, end: parsed.marker.span.start },
      replacement: `${rendered} `,
    };
  }
  const position = parsed.arguments[parsed.arguments.length - 1]?.span.end ?? parsed.marker.span.end;
  return { span: { start: position, end: position }, replacement: ` ${rendered}` };
};

export class LaunchOptions {
  private readonly parsed: ParsedLaunchOptions;

  private constructor(private readonly source: string) {
    this.parsed = parseLaunchOptions(source);
  }

  static parse(source: string): LaunchOptions {
    return new LaunchOptions(source);
  }

  get editable(): boolean {
    return this.parsed.diagnostics.length === 0;
  }

  get diagnostics(): readonly ParseDiagnostic[] {
    return this.parsed.diagnostics;
  }

  toString(): string {
    return this.source;
  }

  isEnabled(definition: LaunchOptionDefinition): boolean {
    if (!this.editable) return false;
    if (definition.kind === "environment") return this.getEnvironment(definition.name) === definition.value;
    return findDefinitionSpans(this.parsed, definition).length > 0;
  }

  setEnabled(definition: LaunchOptionDefinition, enabled: boolean): LaunchOptionsEditResult {
    return this.edit([{ kind: enabled ? "enable" : "disable", definition }]);
  }

  replaceDefinition(previous: LaunchOptionDefinition, next: LaunchOptionDefinition): LaunchOptionsEditResult {
    if (!this.editable) return { ok: false, value: this, error: "document-not-editable" };
    if (!this.isEnabled(previous)) return { ok: true, value: this, changed: false };
    return this.edit([
      { kind: "disable", definition: previous },
      { kind: "enable", definition: next },
    ]);
  }

  getEnvironment(name: string): string | undefined {
    return this.parsed.assignments.filter((assignment) => assignment.name === name).slice(-1)[0]?.value;
  }

  hasEnvironment(name: string): boolean {
    return this.parsed.assignments.some((assignment) => assignment.name === name);
  }

  edit(edits: readonly LaunchOptionsEdit[]): LaunchOptionsEditResult {
    if (!this.editable) return { ok: false, value: this, error: "document-not-editable" };
    let current: LaunchOptions = this;
    let changed = false;
    for (const edit of edits) {
      const result = current.applyOne(edit);
      if (!result.ok) return { ...result, value: this };
      current = result.value;
      changed ||= result.changed;
    }
    return { ok: true, value: current, changed };
  }

  private applyOne(edit: LaunchOptionsEdit): LaunchOptionsEditResult {
    if (!isValidLaunchOption(edit.definition)) {
      return { ok: false, value: this, error: "invalid-definition" };
    }
    if (edit.kind === "disable") {
      const deletions = removeDefinitionEdits(this.parsed, edit.definition);
      if (deletions.length === 0) return { ok: true, value: this, changed: false };
      return this.finish(applyTextEdits(this.source, deletions));
    }
    const matches = findDefinitionSpans(this.parsed, edit.definition);
    if (this.isCanonical(edit.definition, matches.length)) return { ok: true, value: this, changed: false };
    const withoutSlot = applyTextEdits(this.source, replacementDeletionEdits(this.parsed, edit.definition));
    const intermediate = LaunchOptions.parse(withoutSlot);
    if (!intermediate.editable) return { ok: false, value: this, error: "invalid-result" };
    return this.finish(applyTextEdits(withoutSlot, [insertionEdit(intermediate.parsed, edit.definition)]));
  }

  private isCanonical(definition: LaunchOptionDefinition, matchCount: number): boolean {
    if (definition.kind === "prefix") return matchCount > 0;
    if (matchCount !== 1) return false;
    if (definition.kind === "environment") {
      return this.parsed.assignments.filter((assignment) => assignment.name === definition.name).length === 1;
    }
    return this.parsed.arguments.filter((word) => word.raw === definition.flag).length === 1;
  }

  private finish(source: string): LaunchOptionsEditResult {
    const normalized = source.trim() === "%command%" ? "" : source;
    const value = LaunchOptions.parse(normalized);
    if (!value.editable) return { ok: false, value: this, error: "invalid-result" };
    return { ok: true, value, changed: normalized !== this.source };
  }
}

```

`src/domain/parser.ts`:

```ts
// Adapted from CheatDeck (https://github.com/SheffeyG/CheatDeck), GPL-3.0.
export interface SourceSpan {
  start: number;
  end: number;
}

type ParseDiagnosticCode =
  | "unterminated-single-quote"
  | "unterminated-double-quote"
  | "unterminated-expansion"
  | "unterminated-backtick"
  | "trailing-escape"
  | "unsupported-operator"
  | "unsupported-comment"
  | "unsupported-dollar-quote"
  | "unsupported-newline"
  | "missing-command-marker"
  | "multiple-command-markers"
  | "empty-prefix-command";

export interface ParseDiagnostic {
  code: ParseDiagnosticCode;
  span: SourceSpan;
}

interface ParsedWord {
  span: SourceSpan;
  raw: string;
}

interface ParsedAssignment {
  span: SourceSpan;
  name: string;
  value?: string;
}

export interface ParsedPrefix {
  span: SourceSpan;
  separatorBefore?: SourceSpan;
  separatorAfter?: SourceSpan;
  words: readonly ParsedWord[];
}

export interface ParsedLaunchOptions {
  source: string;
  assignments: readonly ParsedAssignment[];
  prefixes: readonly ParsedPrefix[];
  marker?: ParsedWord;
  implicitMarker: boolean;
  arguments: readonly ParsedWord[];
  diagnostics: readonly ParseDiagnostic[];
}

type WordPart =
  | { kind: "bare"; raw: string }
  | { kind: "literal"; value: string }
  | { kind: "dynamic" }
  | { kind: "continuation" };

interface InternalWord extends ParsedWord {
  parts: readonly WordPart[];
}

const span = (start: number, end: number): SourceSpan => ({ start, end });
const operators = new Set(["|", "&", ";", "<", ">", "(", ")"]);
const environmentName = /^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$/;
const shellActiveBare = /[*?[]/;

const scanBalanced = (source: string, start: number): { end: number; closed: boolean } => {
  const opening = source[start + 1];
  const closing = opening === "(" ? ")" : "}";
  let depth = 1;
  let quote = "";
  for (let index = start + 2; index < source.length; index++) {
    const char = source[index];
    if (char === "\\" && quote !== "'") {
      index++;
      continue;
    }
    if (quote) {
      if (char === quote) quote = "";
      continue;
    }
    if (char === "'" || char === '"' || char === "`") quote = char;
    else if (char === opening) depth++;
    else if (char === closing && --depth === 0) return { end: index + 1, closed: true };
  }
  return { end: source.length, closed: false };
};

const scanDoubleQuote = (
  source: string,
  start: number,
  diagnostics: ParseDiagnostic[],
): { end: number; value?: string } => {
  let value = "";
  let dynamic = false;
  for (let index = start + 1; index < source.length; index++) {
    const char = source[index];
    if (char === '"') return { end: index + 1, value: dynamic ? undefined : value };
    if (char === "\\") {
      const next = source[index + 1];
      if (next === undefined) break;
      if (next === "\n") index++;
      else if (next === "$" || next === "`" || next === '"' || next === "\\") {
        value += next;
        index++;
      } else value += char;
    } else {
      if (char === "$" || char === "`") dynamic = true;
      value += char;
    }
  }
  diagnostics.push({ code: "unterminated-double-quote", span: span(start, source.length) });
  return { end: source.length };
};

const literalOf = (parts: readonly WordPart[]): string | undefined => {
  let value = "";
  for (const part of parts) {
    if (part.kind === "dynamic") return undefined;
    if (part.kind === "bare") {
      if ((value.length === 0 && part.raw.startsWith("~")) || shellActiveBare.test(part.raw)) return undefined;
      value += part.raw;
    } else if (part.kind === "literal") value += part.value;
  }
  return value;
};

const scanWord = (
  source: string,
  start: number,
  diagnostics: ParseDiagnostic[],
): { word: InternalWord; end: number } => {
  const parts: WordPart[] = [];
  let index = start;
  let bareStart = start;
  const pushBare = (end: number) => {
    if (bareStart < end) parts.push({ kind: "bare", raw: source.slice(bareStart, end) });
  };

  while (index < source.length) {
    const char = source[index];
    if (char === " " || char === "\t" || char === "\n" || operators.has(char)) break;
    if (char === "$" && (source[index + 1] === "'" || source[index + 1] === '"')) {
      diagnostics.push({ code: "unsupported-dollar-quote", span: span(index, index + 2) });
      index++;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === "\\") {
      pushBare(index);
      const next = source[index + 1];
      if (next === undefined) {
        diagnostics.push({ code: "trailing-escape", span: span(index, index + 1) });
        index++;
      } else if (next === "\n") {
        parts.push({ kind: "continuation" });
        index += 2;
      } else {
        parts.push({ kind: "literal", value: next });
        index += 2;
      }
      bareStart = index;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === "'") {
      pushBare(index);
      const close = source.indexOf("'", index + 1);
      const end = close < 0 ? source.length : close + 1;
      if (close < 0) diagnostics.push({ code: "unterminated-single-quote", span: span(index, end) });
      parts.push({
        kind: "literal",
        value: source.slice(index + 1, close < 0 ? end : close),
      });
      index = end;
      bareStart = index;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '"') {
      pushBare(index);
      const quoted = scanDoubleQuote(source, index, diagnostics);
      parts.push(quoted.value === undefined ? { kind: "dynamic" } : { kind: "literal", value: quoted.value });
      index = quoted.end;
      bareStart = index;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === "`") {
      pushBare(index);
      let end = index + 1;
      while (end < source.length && source[end] !== "`") end += source[end] === "\\" ? 2 : 1;
      const closed = end < source.length;
      end = closed ? end + 1 : source.length;
      if (!closed) diagnostics.push({ code: "unterminated-backtick", span: span(index, end) });
      parts.push({ kind: "dynamic" });
      index = end;
      bareStart = index;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === "$" && (source[index + 1] === "{" || source[index + 1] === "(")) {
      pushBare(index);
      const balanced = scanBalanced(source, index);
      if (!balanced.closed) diagnostics.push({ code: "unterminated-expansion", span: span(index, balanced.end) });
      parts.push({ kind: "dynamic" });
      index = balanced.end;
      bareStart = index;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === "$" && source[index + 1] && /[-A-Za-z0-9_@*#?$!]/.test(source[index + 1])) {
      pushBare(index);
      let end = index + 2;
      if (/[A-Za-z_]/.test(source[index + 1])) while (end < source.length && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(source[end])) end++;
      parts.push({ kind: "dynamic" });
      index = end;
      bareStart = index;
      continue;
    }
    index++;
  }
  pushBare(index);
  const raw = source.slice(start, index);
  return { word: { span: span(start, index), raw, parts }, end: index };
};

const tokenize = (source: string, diagnostics: ParseDiagnostic[]): InternalWord[] => {
  const words: InternalWord[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < source.length; ) {
    const char = source[index];
    if (char === " " || char === "\t") index++;
    else if (char === "\n") {
      diagnostics.push({ code: "unsupported-newline", span: span(index, index + 1) });
      index++;
    } else if (operators.has(char)) {
      let end = index + 1;
      while (end < source.length && operators.has(source[end])) end++;
      diagnostics.push({ code: "unsupported-operator", span: span(index, end) });
      index = end;
    } else if (char === "#") {
      let end = index + 1;
      while (end < source.length && source[end] !== "\n") end++;
      diagnostics.push({ code: "unsupported-comment", span: span(index, end) });
      index = end;
    } else {
      const scanned = scanWord(source, index, diagnostics);
      if (!scanned.word.parts.every((part) => part.kind === "continuation")) words.push(scanned.word);
      index = scanned.end;
    }
  }
  return words;
};

const parseAssignment = (word: InternalWord): ParsedAssignment | undefined => {
  const first = word.parts[0];
  if (first?.kind !== "bare") return undefined;
  const equals = first.raw.indexOf("=");
  if (equals <= 0) return undefined;
  const name = first.raw.slice(0, equals);
  if (!environmentName.test(name)) return undefined;
  const remainder = first.raw.slice(equals + 1);
  const valueParts: WordPart[] = remainder
    ? [{ kind: "bare", raw: remainder }, ...word.parts.slice(1)]
    : [...word.parts.slice(1)];
  return { span: word.span, name, value: literalOf(valueParts) };
};

export const parseLaunchOptions = (source: string): ParsedLaunchOptions => {
  const diagnostics: ParseDiagnostic[] = [];
  const words = tokenize(source, diagnostics);
  if (/^[\t ]*$/.test(source) && diagnostics.length === 0) {
    return { source, assignments: [], prefixes: [], implicitMarker: true, arguments: [], diagnostics };
  }

  const markers = words.filter((word) => word.raw === "%command%");
  if (markers.length !== 1) {
    diagnostics.push({
      code: markers.length === 0 ? "missing-command-marker" : "multiple-command-markers",
      span: markers.length === 0 ? span(0, source.length) : markers[1].span,
    });
  }
  if (diagnostics.length > 0 || markers.length !== 1) {
    return { source, assignments: [], prefixes: [], implicitMarker: false, arguments: [], diagnostics };
  }

  const marker = markers[0];
  const markerIndex = words.indexOf(marker);
  const assignments: ParsedAssignment[] = [];
  const prefixes: ParsedPrefix[] = [];
  let assignmentZone = true;
  let current: InternalWord[] = [];
  let separator: InternalWord | undefined;

  const pushPrefix = () => {
    if (current.length === 0) {
      if (separator) diagnostics.push({ code: "empty-prefix-command", span: separator.span });
      return;
    }
    prefixes.push({
      span: span(current[0].span.start, current[current.length - 1].span.end),
      separatorBefore: separator?.span,
      words: current,
    });
    current = [];
    separator = undefined;
  };

  const beforeMarker = words.slice(0, markerIndex);
  for (let index = 0; index < beforeMarker.length; index++) {
    const word = beforeMarker[index];
    if (word.raw === "--") {
      assignmentZone = false;
      if (current.length === 0) diagnostics.push({ code: "empty-prefix-command", span: word.span });
      pushPrefix();
      if (index === beforeMarker.length - 1 && prefixes.length > 0) {
        prefixes[prefixes.length - 1].separatorAfter = word.span;
        separator = undefined;
        continue;
      }
      separator = word;
      continue;
    }
    const assignment = assignmentZone ? parseAssignment(word) : undefined;
    if (assignment) assignments.push(assignment);
    else {
      assignmentZone = false;
      current.push(word);
    }
  }
  pushPrefix();
  if (diagnostics.length > 0) {
    return { source, assignments: [], prefixes: [], implicitMarker: false, arguments: [], diagnostics };
  }
  return {
    source,
    assignments,
    prefixes,
    marker,
    implicitMarker: false,
    arguments: words.slice(markerIndex + 1),
    diagnostics,
  };
};

export const parseRawWords = (source: string): readonly [string, ...string[]] | undefined => {
  if (source.trim() !== source || source.length === 0) return undefined;
  const diagnostics: ParseDiagnostic[] = [];
  const words = tokenize(source, diagnostics);
  if (diagnostics.length > 0 || words.length === 0) return undefined;
  return words.map((word) => word.raw) as [string, ...string[]];
};

export const parseLiteralWord = (source: string): string | undefined => {
  if (source.trim() !== source || source.length === 0) return undefined;
  const diagnostics: ParseDiagnostic[] = [];
  const words = tokenize(source, diagnostics);
  if (diagnostics.length > 0 || words.length !== 1) return undefined;
  return literalOf(words[0].parts);
};

```

`src/domain/sidecar.ts`:

```ts
// Launch-option editing adapted from CheatDeck (GPL-3.0).
import { LaunchOptions, LaunchOptionDefinition } from "./options";
import { parseLiteralWord } from "./parser";

const command = "PROTON_REMOTE_DEBUG_CMD";
const filesystem = "PRESSURE_VESSEL_FILESYSTEMS_RW";
const environment = (name: string, value: string): LaunchOptionDefinition => ({ kind: "environment", name, value });
export const sidecarPath = (source: string) => {
  const value = LaunchOptions.parse(source).getEnvironment(command);
  return value === undefined ? undefined : parseLiteralWord(value);
};
export function editSidecar(source: string, path?: string): string {
  const options = LaunchOptions.parse(source);
  if (!options.editable) throw new Error("These launch options cannot be edited safely. Review them in Steam Properties.");
  if (options.hasEnvironment(filesystem) && options.getEnvironment(filesystem) === undefined)
    throw new Error("The shared-folder setting contains a dynamic value and cannot be edited safely.");
  const edits: Parameters<LaunchOptions["edit"]>[0][number][] = [
    { kind: "disable", definition: environment(command, "") },
  ];
  if (path) {
    if (!path.startsWith("/") || /[\x00-\x1f:]/.test(path)) throw new Error("Choose a valid absolute Linux program path without colons.");
    const directory = path.slice(0, path.lastIndexOf("/")) || "/";
    const existing = options.getEnvironment(filesystem);
    const directories = existing ? existing.split(":") : [];
    if (!directories.includes(directory)) directories.push(directory);
    edits.push({ kind: "enable", definition: environment(command, `'${path.split("'").join(`'"'"'`)}'`) });
    edits.push({ kind: "enable", definition: environment(filesystem, directories.join(":")) });
  }
  // Keep shared directories on disable: another launch tool may also use them.
  const result = options.edit(edits);
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error("Could not update launch options safely.");
  return result.value.toString();
}

export function readLaunchOptions(appId: string): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let subscription: { unregister: () => void } | undefined;
    let settled = false;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      settled = true; subscription?.unregister(); reject(new Error("Steam did not return the game's launch options."));
    }, 5000);
    try {
      subscription = SteamClient.Apps.RegisterForAppDetails(Number(appId), details => {
        if (settled || details?.strLaunchOptions === undefined) return;
        settled = true; window.clearTimeout(timer); subscription?.unregister(); resolve(details.strLaunchOptions);
      });
      if (settled) subscription.unregister();
    } catch (error) {
      settled = true; window.clearTimeout(timer); subscription?.unregister(); reject(error);
    }
  });
}

export async function saveSidecar(appId: string, expected: string, path?: string) {
  const current = await readLaunchOptions(appId);
  if (current !== expected) throw new Error("Launch options changed elsewhere. Refresh this page and try again.");
  const next = editSidecar(current, path);
  SteamClient.Apps.SetAppLaunchOptions(Number(appId), next);
  // Confirm Steam has published the write before reporting success.
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 4; attempt++) {
    const saved = await readLaunchOptions(appId);
    if (saved === next) return saved;
    await new Promise(resolve => window.setTimeout(resolve, 200));
  }
  throw new Error("Steam has not confirmed the change. Refresh before trying again.");
}

```

`src/hooks/useDownloads.ts`:

```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { DownloadJob, getDownloads } from "../utils/backend";
import { invalidateLibrary } from "./useLibrary";

let jobs: DownloadJob[] = [];
let timer: number | undefined;
let generation = 0;
let request = 0;
let completed = "";
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const publish = (next: DownloadJob[]) => { jobs = next; listeners.forEach((listener) => listener()); };

async function poll(epoch: number) {
  const id = ++request;
  try {
    const next = await getDownloads();
    if (epoch !== generation || id !== request) return;
    publish(next);
    const signature = next.filter((job) => job.state === "done").map((job) => job.id).sort().join(",");
    if (signature !== completed) {
      completed = signature;
      invalidateLibrary();
    }
  } catch {
    // Temporary RPC errors do not stop synchronization.
  } finally {
    if (epoch === generation && listeners.size) timer = window.setTimeout(() => { void poll(epoch); }, 1500);
  }
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void) {
  listeners.add(listener);
  if (listeners.size === 1) void poll(++generation);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
    if (!listeners.size) { generation++; window.clearTimeout(timer); }
  };
}

export function trackDownload(job: DownloadJob) {
  request++; // Discard a poll response started before this action.
  publish([...jobs.filter((item) => item.id !== job.id), job]);
}

export const useDownloads = () => useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, () => jobs);

```

`src/hooks/useLibrary.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { getLibrary, LibraryResponse } from "../utils/backend";

const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
export const invalidateLibrary = () => listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());

export function useLibrary(appId: string, name: string) {
  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<LibraryResponse | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const sequence = useRef(0);
  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    const request = ++sequence.current;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await getLibrary(appId, name);
      if (request !== sequence.current) return;
      setLibrary(response);
      setError(response.success ? null : response.error || "library_unavailable");
    } catch {
      if (request === sequence.current) {
        setLibrary(null);
        setError("library_unavailable");
      }
    } finally {
      if (request === sequence.current) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [appId, name]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const invalidate = () => { void refresh(); };
    listeners.add(invalidate);
    invalidate();
    return () => { listeners.delete(invalidate); sequence.current++; };
  }, [refresh]);
  return { library, loading, error, refresh };
}

import { routerHook } from "@decky/api";
import { definePlugin, staticClasses } from "@decky/ui";
import { FaGamepad as PluginIcon } from "react-icons/fa";

import installGameMenu from "./patch";
import { initLanguage, PLUGIN_NAME } from "./utils/i18n";
import { GAME_PAGE_ROUTE } from "./utils/routes";
import GamePage from "./views/GamePage";
import Settings from "./views/Settings";

export default definePlugin(() => {
  initLanguage();
  const removeGameMenu = installGameMenu();

  routerHook.addRoute(GAME_PAGE_ROUTE, GamePage, { exact: true });

  return {
    name: PLUGIN_NAME,
    title: <div className={staticClasses.Title}>{PLUGIN_NAME}</div>,
    content: <Settings />,
    icon: <PluginIcon />,
    onDismount() {
      routerHook.removeRoute(GAME_PAGE_ROUTE);
      removeGameMenu();
    },
  };
});

`src/patch.tsx`:

```tsx
/**
 * Adapted from CheatDeck (https://github.com/SheffeyG/CheatDeck/blob/main/src/patch.tsx),
 * itself credited to decky-steamgriddb's contextMenuPatch.tsx. Same react-patching
 * technique, renamed for the "DeckyGCM" menu item and its own route.
 */

import {
  afterPatch,
  type Export,
  fakeRenderComponent,
  findInReactTree,
  findInTree,
  findModuleByExport,
  MenuItem,
  Navigation,
  type Patch,
} from "@decky/ui";
import type { FC } from "react";

import { menuItemLabel } from "./utils/i18n";
import { gamePageRoute } from "./utils/routes";

const MENU_ITEM_KEY = "game-cheats-manager";

// Always add before "Properties..."
const spliceMenuItem = (children: any[], appid: number) => {
  const propertiesMenuItemIdx = children.findIndex((item) =>
    findInReactTree(item, (x) => x?.onSelected?.toString().includes("AppProperties")),
  );
  children.splice(
    propertiesMenuItemIdx < 0 ? children.length : propertiesMenuItemIdx,
    0,
    <MenuItem
      key={MENU_ITEM_KEY}
      onSelected={() => {
        Navigation.Navigate(gamePageRoute(appid));
      }}
    >
      {menuItemLabel()}
    </MenuItem>,
  );
};

// Check if correct menu by looking at the code of the onSelected function.
// Should be enough to ignore the screenshots and other menus.
const isOpeningAppContextMenu = (items: any[]) => {
  if (!items?.length) {
    return false;
  }
  return !!findInReactTree(items, (x) => x?.props?.onSelected?.toString().includes("launchSource"));
};

const handleItemDupes = (items: any[]) => {
  const existingIdx = items.findIndex((x: any) => x?.key === MENU_ITEM_KEY);
  if (existingIdx !== -1) items.splice(existingIdx, 1);
};

const patchMenuItems = (menuItems: any[], appid: number) => {
  let updatedAppid: number = appid;
  // find the first menu component that has the correct appid, sometimes the one passed is cached from another context menu
  const parentOverview = menuItems.find(
    (x: any) => x?._owner?.pendingProps?.overview?.appid && x._owner.pendingProps.overview.appid !== appid,
  );
  if (parentOverview) {
    updatedAppid = parentOverview._owner.pendingProps.overview.appid;
  }
  if (updatedAppid === appid) {
    const foundApp = findInTree(menuItems, (x) => x?.app?.appid, { walkable: ["props", "children"] });
    if (foundApp) {
      updatedAppid = foundApp.app.appid;
    }
  }
  spliceMenuItem(menuItems, updatedAppid);
};

/** Install lazily: a Steam menu change must not prevent the sidebar loading. */
export default function installGameMenu(): () => void {
  const patches: Patch[] = [];
  const prototypes = new WeakSet<object>();
  const components = new WeakSet<object>();
  let currentAppId = 0;
  const cleanup = () => { for (const patch of patches.splice(0).reverse()) patch.unpatch(); };
  const update = (items: any[], appid: number) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(items) || !isOpeningAppContextMenu(items)) return;
    handleItemDupes(items);
    patchMenuItems(items, appid);
  };
  try {
    const module = findModuleByExport((e: Export) => e?.toString?.().includes("().LibraryContextMenu"));
    const factory = Object.values(module || {}).find((sibling) => sibling?.toString().includes("navigator:"));
    if (!factory) return cleanup;
    const LibraryContextMenu = fakeRenderComponent(factory as FC)?.type;
    if (!LibraryContextMenu?.prototype?.render) return cleanup;
    patches.push(afterPatch(LibraryContextMenu.prototype, "render", (_: any, component: any) => {
      const overviewId = component?._owner?.pendingProps?.overview?.appid;
      const found = findInTree(component?.props?.children, (x) => x?.app?.appid, { walkable: ["props", "children"] });
      const appid = overviewId ?? found?.app?.appid;
      if (!appid) return component;
      currentAppId = appid;
      if (!components.has(component) && typeof component.type === "function") {
        components.add(component);
        patches.push(afterPatch(component, "type", (_: any, ret: any) => {
          const proto = ret?.type?.prototype;
          if (!proto?.render || prototypes.has(proto)) return ret;
          prototypes.add(proto);
          patches.push(afterPatch(proto, "render", (_: any, rendered: any) => {
            update(rendered?.props?.children?.[0], currentAppId);
            return rendered;
          }));
          if (proto.shouldComponentUpdate) patches.push(afterPatch(proto, "shouldComponentUpdate", ([next]: any, result: any) => {
            if (result === true) update(next?.children, currentAppId);
            return result;
          }));
          return ret;
        }));
      }
      update(component?.props?.children, appid);
      return component;
    }));
  } catch {
    cleanup();
  }
  return cleanup;
}

```

`src/utils/backend.ts`:

```ts
import { callable } from "@decky/api";

export interface DatabaseStatus {
  configured: boolean;
  data_source?: { configured: boolean; state: string };
  ready: boolean;
  // file name -> last-modified (unix seconds), null when not downloaded yet
  files: Record<string, number | null>;
}

export interface RefreshResult {
  success: boolean;
  errors: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface TrainerResult {
  id: string;
  trainer_name: string;
  game_name: string;
  origin: string;
  version: string;
}

export interface SearchResponse {
  success: boolean;
  error?: "keyword_too_short" | "database_missing" | string;
  results: TrainerResult[];
}

export type DownloadState = "queued" | "downloading" | "extracting" | "done" | "error" | "cancelled";

export interface DownloadJob {
  id: string;
  trainer_id: string;
  directory: string;
  app_ids: string[];
  trainer_name: string;
  state: DownloadState;
  downloaded: number;
  total: number;
  path: string | null;
  error: string | null;
}

export const getDatabaseStatus = callable<[], DatabaseStatus>("get_database_status");
export const refreshDatabase = callable<[], RefreshResult>("refresh_database");
export const searchTrainers = callable<[keyword: string, lang: string], SearchResponse>("search_trainers");
export const downloadTrainer = callable<[trainer_id: string, app_id: string, expected_directory: string], { success: boolean; error?: string; job?: DownloadJob }>(
  "download_trainer",
);
export const getDownloads = callable<[], DownloadJob[]>("get_downloads");

export interface LocalTrainer {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  path: string;
  files: string[];
  trainer_id: string;
  game_name: string;
  app_ids: string[];
  version: string;
  origin: string;
}

export interface LibraryResponse {
  success: boolean;
  directory?: string;
  game_name?: string;
  trainers?: LocalTrainer[];
  error?: string;
}

export const getLibrary = callable<[app_id: string, game_name: string], LibraryResponse>("get_library");
export const setLibraryDirectory = callable<[path: string], LibraryResponse>("set_library_directory");

export const getLibrarySettings = callable<[], { directory: string; database: DatabaseStatus }>("get_library_settings");
export const manageTrainer = callable<[
  action: string, app_id: string, expected_directory: string, entry_id: string,
  filename: string, source: string, game_name: string,
], { success: boolean; error?: string; path?: string; filename?: string; job?: DownloadJob }>("manage_trainer");

`src/utils/i18n.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { en, zhCN, zhTW, Translations } from "./locales";

export const PLUGIN_NAME = "DeckyGCM";
export type Locale = "en" | "zh-CN" | "zh-TW";
let currentLocale: Locale = "en";
let pending: Promise<void> | undefined;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => { listeners.delete(listener); };
};

export function steamLocale(language: unknown): Locale {
  if (typeof language !== "string") return "en";
  const normalized = language.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, "-");
  if (["schinese", "zh-cn", "zh-sg", "zh-hans", "zh-hans-cn", "zh"].includes(normalized)) return "zh-CN";
  if (["tchinese", "zh-tw", "zh-hk", "zh-mo", "zh-hant", "zh-hant-tw", "zh-hant-hk"].includes(normalized)) return "zh-TW";
  return "en";
}

export function initLanguage(): Promise<void> {
  if (pending) return pending;
  pending = (async () => {
    try {
      const next = steamLocale(await SteamClient.Settings.GetCurrentLanguage());
      if (next !== currentLocale) {
        currentLocale = next;
        listeners.forEach(listener => listener());
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep the last detected language if Steam is temporarily unavailable.
    }
  })().finally(() => { pending = undefined; });
  return pending;
}

export function getLanguage(): Locale { return currentLocale; }
// GCM's catalog worker supports English and Chinese, independent of UI script.
export function getSearchLanguage(): "en" | "zh" { return currentLocale === "en" ? "en" : "zh"; }
export function translations(locale: Locale = currentLocale): Translations {
  return locale === "zh-CN" ? zhCN : locale === "zh-TW" ? zhTW : en;
}
export function useTranslations(): Translations {
  const locale = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getLanguage);
  useEffect(() => { void initLanguage(); }, []);
  return translations(locale);
}
export const menuItemLabel = () => PLUGIN_NAME;

// Older backend errors remain English or machine codes. Translate known values
// at the presentation boundary without changing identifiers or diagnostic logs.
const errorKeys: Record<string, keyof Translations> = {
  library_unavailable: "libraryUnavailable", library_changed: "libraryChanged",
  invalid_app_id: "invalidAppId", unknown_trainer: "trainerMissing",
  plugin_stopping: "stopping", keyword_too_short: "keywordTooShort", database_missing: "databaseMissing",
  "Trainer no longer exists. Refresh the library.": "trainerMissing",
  "Unknown trainer file.": "unknownFile",
  "Choose an .exe or .bat program. Cheat Tables require Cheat Engine.": "executableRequired",
  "Choose an .exe, .bat or .CT file.": "selectFile",
  "This trainer is shared with another game and cannot be deleted here.": "sharedTrainer",
  "Trainer is outside the current library.": "outsideLibrary",
  "Only trainers downloaded or imported by this plugin can be deleted.": "unmanagedTrainer",
  "Unknown download for this game.": "unknownDownload",
  "Choose an existing folder.": "invalidFolder", "Choose a folder, not a file.": "folderRequired",
  "No .exe or .CT trainer files found in the download.": "noTrainerFiles",
  "No GCM data source configured": "dataSourceMissing",
  "Could not load GCM authentication configuration": "dataSourceInvalid",
  "GCM device authentication failed": "authenticationFailed",
  "Could not connect to GCM server": "connectionFailed",
  "Could not connect to server": "connectionFailed",
  "Server request timed out": "connectionFailed",
  "Download stream interrupted": "connectionFailed",
  "GCM server request failed": "serverError",
  "GCM request rate limited": "rateLimited",
  "GCM server rate limit reached": "rateLimited",
  "GCM enrollment is waiting before retrying": "connectionFailed",
  unconfigured: "dataSourceMissing", invalid_config: "dataSourceInvalid", auth_failed: "authenticationFailed",
  connection_failed: "connectionFailed", rate_limited: "rateLimited", server_error: "serverError",
  "Invalid trainer database format": "invalidDatabase",
  "7z/RAR extraction requires libarchive on this device.": "missingDependency",
  "Unsafe path in trainer archive.": "invalidArchive",
  "Trainer archive contains too many files.": "invalidArchive",
  "Trainer archive is too large.": "invalidArchive",
  "Links and special files are not allowed in trainer archives.": "invalidArchive",
  "Trainer archive has no file content.": "invalidArchive",
};

export function errorMessage(error: unknown, t: Translations = translations()): string {
  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : typeof error === "string" ? error : "";
  const key = (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(en, message) ? message as keyof Translations : undefined)
    || errorKeys[message] || (Object.keys(en) as (keyof Translations)[]).find(key => en[key] === message);
  const value = key && t[key];
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : t.unknownError;
}
