`src/utils/routes.ts`:

```ts
export const GAME_PAGE_ROUTE = "/game-cheats-manager/:appid";

export const gamePageRoute = (appid: number | string) => `/game-cheats-manager/${appid}`;

// routerHook passes react-router v5-style props, but the exact shape isn't
// part of @decky/ui's stable public types, so the appid is read straight out
// of the URL instead of trusting a particular prop shape across versions.
export function getAppIdFromLocation(): string | null {
  const match = window.location.pathname.match(/\/game-cheats-manager\/(\d+)/);
  return match ? match[1] : null;
}

```

`src/views/BindTrainer.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslations, errorMessage } from "../utils/i18n";
import { FileSelectionType, openFilePicker } from "@decky/api";
import { ButtonItem, PanelSection, PanelSectionRow } from "@decky/ui";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useLibrary, invalidateLibrary } from "../hooks/useLibrary";
import { manageTrainer } from "../utils/backend";

export default function BindTrainer({ appId, gameName }: { appId: string; gameName: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations();
  const { library, error } = useLibrary(appId, gameName);
  const [source, setSource] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  async function browse() {
    try {
      const picked = await openFilePicker(FileSelectionType.FILE, library?.directory || "~", true, true,
        undefined, ["exe", "bat", "ct"]);
      setSource(picked.realpath || picked.path); setMessage("");
    } catch { /* Closing the picker leaves the current selection unchanged. */ }
  }
  async function bind() {
    setBusy(true); setMessage("");
    try {
      const result = await manageTrainer("import", appId, library?.directory || "", "", "", source, gameName);
      if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error);
      invalidateLibrary(); setSource("");
      setMessage("bound");
    } catch (error) { setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknownError"); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <PanelSection>
    <PanelSectionRow><div>{t.bindHint}</div></PanelSectionRow>
    <PanelSectionRow><ButtonItem layout="below" label={t.localTrainer} description={source || t.selectFile}
      disabled={busy || !library?.success} onClick={() => { void browse(); }}>{t.browseFiles}</ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>
    <PanelSectionRow><div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: .7, overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{t.destination}: {library?.directory || t.loading}<br />
      {t.copyHint}</div></PanelSectionRow>
    <PanelSectionRow><ButtonItem layout="below" disabled={busy || !source || !library?.success} onClick={() => { void bind(); }}>
      {busy ? t.copying : t.copyAndBind}</ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>
    {(message || error) && <PanelSectionRow><div role="status" style={{ overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{errorMessage(message || error, t)}</div></PanelSectionRow>}
  </PanelSection>;
}

```

`src/views/Downloaded.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslations, errorMessage } from "../utils/i18n";
import { ButtonItem, ConfirmModal, Focusable, PanelSection, PanelSectionRow, showModal, Spinner } from "@decky/ui";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useLibrary, invalidateLibrary } from "../hooks/useLibrary";
import { useDownloads, trackDownload } from "../hooks/useDownloads";
import { LocalTrainer, manageTrainer } from "../utils/backend";
import { readLaunchOptions, saveSidecar, sidecarPath } from "../domain/sidecar";

export default function Downloaded({ appId, gameName }: { appId: string; gameName: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations();
  const { library, loading, error, refresh } = useLibrary(appId, gameName);
  const jobs = useDownloads().filter(job => job.app_ids.includes(appId) && job.state !== "done");
  const [options, setOptions] = useState<string>();
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState("");
  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(new Set<string>());
  const selected = options === undefined ? undefined : sidecarPath(options);
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    readLaunchOptions(appId).then(value => { if (active) setOptions(value); }).catch(error => { if (active) setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknownError"); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [appId]);
  async function action(run: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true); setMessage("");
    try { await run(); }
    catch (error) { setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknownError"); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  async function setSidecar(trainer: LocalTrainer, file: string) {
    if (options === undefined) throw new Error("Launch options are not loaded yet.");
    const result = await manageTrainer("executable", appId, library?.directory || "", trainer.id, file, "", "");
    if (!result.success || !result.path) throw new Error(result.error);
    setOptions(await saveSidecar(appId, options, result.path));
    setMessage("sidecarSaved");
  }
  function remove(trainer: LocalTrainer) {
    showModal(<ConfirmModal strTitle={t.deleteTitle} strDescription={t.deleteDescription(trainer.name)}
      strOKButtonText={t.delete} strCancelButtonText={t.cancel} onOK={() => { void action(async () => {
        const prefix = trainer.path.endsWith("/") ? trainer.path : `${trainer.path}/`;
        if (selected && (selected === trainer.path || selected.startsWith(prefix)))
          throw new Error("Disable this trainer's sidecar before deleting it.");
        const result = await manageTrainer("delete", appId, library?.directory || "", trainer.id, "", "", "");
        if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error);
        invalidateLibrary(); setMessage("trainerDeleted");
      }); }} />, window);
  }
  return <Focusable>
    <PanelSection title={t.sidecar}>
      <PanelSectionRow><div style={{ overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{selected || t.noSidecar}</div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: .7 }}>{t.sidecarHint}</div></PanelSectionRow>
      {selected && <PanelSectionRow><ButtonItem layout="below" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { void action(async () => {
        setOptions(await saveSidecar(appId, options!)); setMessage("sidecarDisabled");
      }); }}>{t.disableSidecar}</ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>}
      <PanelSectionRow><ButtonItem layout="below" disabled={busy || loading} onClick={() => { void action(async () => {
        await refresh(); setOptions(await readLaunchOptions(appId));
      }); }}>{t.refresh}</ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>
      {(message || error) && <PanelSectionRow><div role="status" style={{ overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{errorMessage(message || error, t)}</div></PanelSectionRow>}
    </PanelSection>
    {jobs.length > 0 && <PanelSection title={t.downloadActivity}>{jobs.map(job => {
      const active = ["queued", "downloading", "extracting"].includes(job.state);
      const percent = job.total ? Math.floor(job.downloaded / job.total * 100) : null;
      return <PanelSectionRow key={job.id}>
        <div>{job.trainer_name}</div>
        <div role="status" style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: .7, overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>
          {job.state === "downloading" ? t.downloading(percent) : job.state === "error" ? t.failed : t[job.state]}
          <br />{job.directory}{job.error && <div>{errorMessage(job.error, t)}</div>}</div>
        {active && job.total > 0 && <progress aria-label={t.downloadProgress} value={job.downloaded} max={job.total} style={{ width: "100%" }} />}
        {active && <ButtonItem layout="below" disabled={busy || cancelling.has(job.id)} onClick={() => { void action(async () => {
          const result = await manageTrainer("cancel", appId, "", job.id, "", "", "");
          if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error);
          setCancelling(previous => new Set(previous).add(job.id));
          if (result.job) trackDownload(result.job);
          setMessage("cancellationRequested");
        }); }}>{cancelling.has(job.id) ? t.cancelling : t.cancelDownload}</ButtonItem>}
      </PanelSectionRow>;
    })}</PanelSection>}
    <PanelSection title={t.downloadedTitle(library?.trainers?.length || 0)}>
      {loading && <Spinner style={{ height: 32 }} />}
      {!loading && !library?.trainers?.length && <PanelSectionRow>{t.noTrainers}</PanelSectionRow>}
      {library?.trainers?.map(trainer => {
        const programs = trainer.files.filter(file => /\.(exe|bat)$/i.test(file));
        return <PanelSectionRow key={trainer.id}>
          <ButtonItem layout="below" label={trainer.name} description={[trainer.version, trainer.origin === "Local file" ? t.localFile : trainer.origin].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
            onClick={() => setExpanded(expanded === trainer.id ? "" : trainer.id)}>{expanded === trainer.id ? t.hideActions : t.manage}</ButtonItem>
          {expanded === trainer.id && <>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: .7, overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{trainer.path}</div>
            {programs.map(file => {
              const current = selected === trainer.path || selected === `${trainer.path}/${file}`;
              return <ButtonItem key={file} layout="below" label={file} disabled={busy || options === undefined || current}
                onClick={() => { void action(() => setSidecar(trainer, file)); }}>{current ? t.selectedSidecar : t.useSidecar}</ButtonItem>;
            })}
            {!programs.length && <div style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>{t.cheatEngineRequired}</div>}
            <ButtonItem layout="below" disabled={busy || options === undefined} onClick={() => remove(trainer)}>{t.deleteTrainer}</ButtonItem>
          </>}
        </PanelSectionRow>;
      })}
    </PanelSection>
  </Focusable>;
}
```

```tsx
import { useTranslations } from "../utils/i18n";
import { SidebarNavigation } from "@decky/ui";
import { FaSearch, FaDownload, FaLink } from "react-icons/fa";
import { getAppIdFromLocation } from "../utils/routes";
import TrainerSearch from "./TrainerSearch";
import Downloaded from "./Downloaded";
import BindTrainer from "./BindTrainer";

export default function GamePage() {
  const t = useTranslations();
  const appId = getAppIdFromLocation();
  if (!appId) return <div>{t.noAppId}</div>;
  const name = typeof appStore === "undefined" ? "" : appStore?.GetAppOverviewByAppID?.(Number(appId))?.display_name || "";
  return <SidebarNavigation key={appId} title={name || t.gameTitle(appId)} showTitle pages={[
    { identifier: "search", title: t.search, icon: <FaSearch />, content: <TrainerSearch appId={appId} initialKeyword={name} /> },
    { identifier: "downloaded", title: t.downloaded, icon: <FaDownload />, content: <Downloaded appId={appId} gameName={name} /> },
    { identifier: "bind", title: t.bindTrainer, icon: <FaLink />, content: <BindTrainer appId={appId} gameName={name} /> },
  ]} />;
```

`src/views/Settings.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslations, errorMessage } from "../utils/i18n";
import { FileSelectionType, openFilePicker } from "@decky/api";
import { ButtonItem, PanelSection, PanelSectionRow } from "@decky/ui";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { getLibrarySettings, refreshDatabase, setLibraryDirectory, DatabaseStatus } from "../utils/backend";
import { invalidateLibrary } from "../hooks/useLibrary";

export default function Settings() {
  const t = useTranslations();
  const [directory, setDirectory] = useState("");
  const [database, setDatabase] = useState<DatabaseStatus>();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    getLibrarySettings().then(value => {
      if (active) { setDirectory(value.directory); setDatabase(value.database); }
    }).catch(() => { if (active) setMessage("settingsFailed"); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, []);
  const choose = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    let path: string;
    try {
      const picked = await openFilePicker(FileSelectionType.FOLDER, directory || "~", false, true);
      path = picked.realpath || picked.path;
    } catch { setBusy(false); return; }
    try {
      const result = await setLibraryDirectory(path);
      if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error);
      setDirectory(result.directory || path);
      invalidateLibrary();
      setMessage("folderSaved");
    } catch (error) { setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknownError"); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };
  const update = async () => {
    setBusy(true); setMessage("updatingDatabase");
    try {
      const result = await refreshDatabase();
      setDatabase((await getLibrarySettings()).database);
      setMessage(result.success ? "databaseUpdated" : Object.values(result.errors)[0] || "databaseUpdateFailed");
    } catch { setMessage("databaseUpdateFailed"); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };
  return <PanelSection title={t.settings}>
    <PanelSectionRow><ButtonItem layout="below" label={t.folder} description={directory || t.loading}
      disabled={busy || !directory} onClick={() => { void choose(); }}>{t.chooseFolder}</ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>
    <PanelSectionRow><div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: .7 }}>{t.folderHint}</div></PanelSectionRow>
    <PanelSectionRow><ButtonItem layout="below" label={t.catalog}
      description={database?.ready ? t.catalogReady : t.catalogMissing}
      disabled={busy || !database} onClick={() => { void update(); }}>{t.updateDatabase}</ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>
    {message && <PanelSectionRow><div role="status" style={{ fontSize: 13, overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{errorMessage(message, t)}</div></PanelSectionRow>}
  </PanelSection>;
}
```

`src/views/TrainerSearch.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslations, errorMessage, initLanguage, getSearchLanguage } from "../utils/i18n";
import { ButtonItem, PanelSection, PanelSectionRow, Spinner, TextField } from "@decky/ui";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { downloadTrainer, searchTrainers, TrainerResult } from "../utils/backend";
import { useLibrary, invalidateLibrary } from "../hooks/useLibrary";
import { trackDownload, useDownloads } from "../hooks/useDownloads";

export default function TrainerSearch({ appId, initialKeyword }: { appId: string; initialKeyword: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations();
  const { library, error } = useLibrary(appId, initialKeyword);
  const jobs = useDownloads();
  const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState(initialKeyword);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<TrainerResult[]>([]);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [starting, setStarting] = useState(new Set<string>());
  const sequence = useRef(0);
  const initialized = useRef(false);
  async function search(query: string) {
    if (!query.trim()) return;
    const request = ++sequence.current;
    setSearching(true); setMessage(""); setResults([]);
    try {
      await initLanguage();
      if (request !== sequence.current) return;
      const response = await searchTrainers(query, getSearchLanguage());
      if (request !== sequence.current) return;
      if (!response.success) setMessage(response.error || "searchFailed");
      else { setResults(response.results); if (!response.results.length) setMessage("noResults"); }
    } catch { if (request === sequence.current) setMessage("searchFailed"); }
    finally { if (request === sequence.current) setSearching(false); }
  }
  useEffect(() => {
    const name = initialKeyword || library?.game_name;
    if (name && !initialized.current) { initialized.current = true; setKeyword(name); void search(name); }
  }, [initialKeyword, library?.game_name]);
  useEffect(() => () => { sequence.current++; }, []);
  async function download(trainer: TrainerResult) {
    setStarting(previous => new Set(previous).add(trainer.id));
    try {
      const response = await downloadTrainer(trainer.id, appId, library?.directory || "");
      if (!response.success || !response.job) {
        if (response.error === "library_changed") invalidateLibrary();
        throw new Error(response.error || "Could not start download.");
      }
      trackDownload(response.job);
      setMessage("downloadStarted");
    } catch (error) { setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknownError"); }
    finally { setStarting(previous => { const next = new Set(previous); next.delete(trainer.id); return next; }); }
  }
  return <PanelSection>
    <PanelSectionRow><TextField label={t.gameName} value={keyword} onChange={event => setKeyword(event.target.value)}
      onKeyDown={event => { if (event.key === "Enter" && !searching) void search(keyword); }} /></PanelSectionRow>
    <PanelSectionRow><ButtonItem layout="below" disabled={searching || !keyword.trim()} onClick={() => { void search(keyword); }}>
      {searching ? t.searching : t.search}</ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>
    {searching && <Spinner style={{ height: 32 }} />}
    {(message || error) && <PanelSectionRow><div role="status">{errorMessage(message || error, t)}</div></PanelSectionRow>}
    {results.map(trainer => {
      const installed = library?.trainers?.some(item => item.trainer_id === trainer.id);
      const job = jobs.find(item => item.trainer_id === trainer.id && item.directory === library?.directory
        && ["queued", "downloading", "extracting"].includes(item.state));
      return <PanelSectionRow key={trainer.id}><ButtonItem layout="below" label={trainer.trainer_name}
        description={[trainer.version, trainer.origin === "Local file" ? t.localFile : trainer.origin].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
        disabled={installed || Boolean(job) || starting.has(trainer.id) || !library?.success}
        onClick={() => { void download(trainer); }}>
        {installed ? t.downloaded : job ? t.downloading(null) : starting.has(trainer.id) ? t.starting : t.download}
      </ButtonItem></PanelSectionRow>;
    })}
  </PanelSection>;
}
```

```py
"""
Minimal stand-in for the real `decky` module (backend/decky_loader/plugin/imports/decky.pyi
in SteamDeckHomebrew/decky-loader), which only exists inside an actual Decky Loader
runtime. This lets CI import main.py and every py_modules/*.py file — and therefore
catch syntax errors, typos, and bad cross-module references — without one.
"""
```

`tests/dev.test.mjs`:

```mjs
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { copyRuntime, loadDevEnv } from "../scripts/dev.mjs";

test("development payload includes generated auth but excludes raw secrets and host wheels", () => {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "gcm-dev-test-"));
  const source = path.join(root, "source");
  const target = path.join(root, "target");
  try {
    for (const name of ["dist/index.js", "main.py", "package.json", "plugin.json", "py_modules/backend.py", "py_modules/_gcm_core/auth.py", "py_modules/secret_config.py", "py_modules/_vendor/windows.pyd", "py_modules/__pycache__/backend.pyc"]) {
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(path.join(source, name)), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(source, name), "test");
    }
    copyRuntime(source, target);
    assert(fs.existsSync(path.join(target, "py_modules/_gcm_core/auth.py")));
    assert(!fs.existsSync(path.join(target, "py_modules/secret_config.py")));
    assert(!fs.existsSync(path.join(target, "py_modules/_vendor")));
    assert(!fs.existsSync(path.join(target, "py_modules/__pycache__")));
  } finally {
    assert.equal(path.dirname(fs.realpathSync(root)), fs.realpathSync(os.tmpdir()));
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});

test("local environment stays private and process variables override it", () => {
  const directory = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "gcm-env-test-"));
  try {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(directory, ".env"), 'DECKY_HOST="local.invalid"\nDECKY_PORT=2200\n');
    assert.deepEqual(loadDevEnv(directory, { DECKY_PORT: "2300" }), {
      DECKY_HOST: "local.invalid", DECKY_PORT: "2300",
    });
  } finally {
    assert.equal(path.dirname(fs.realpathSync(directory)), fs.realpathSync(os.tmpdir()));
    fs.rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});
```

`tests/fixtures/auth_config.py`:

```py
# Inert GCM signing fixture, parsed by the adapter; never imported directly.
ENROLL_ENDPOINT = 'https://example.invalid/enroll_endpoint'
SIGNED_URL_DOWNLOAD_ENDPOINT = 'https://example.invalid/signed_url_download_endpoint'
SIGNED_URL_UPLOAD_ENDPOINT = 'https://example.invalid/signed_url_upload_endpoint'
VERSION_CHECKER_ENDPOINT = 'https://example.invalid/version_checker_endpoint'
PATCH_PATTERNS_ENDPOINT = 'https://example.invalid/patch_patterns_endpoint'
CLIENT_API_KEY = 'TEST_ONLY_NOT_A_REAL_KEY'
_ENROLL_TIMEOUT = (5, 15)
_RETRY_INTERVAL = 3600
_UNKNOWN_CREDENTIAL = 401
_REQUEST_ATTEMPTS = 3

def _enroll():
    """Trade this install's details for a credential the server generates."""
    if not ENROLL_ENDPOINT or not CLIENT_API_KEY:
        return None
    from config import APP_VERSION, settings
    payload = {'app': 'GCM', 'appVersion': APP_VERSION, 'platform': 'win32', 'language': settings.get('language'), 'machineHash': _machine_hash()}
    try:
        response = requests.get(ENROLL_ENDPOINT, headers={'x-api-key': CLIENT_API_KEY}, params=payload, timeout=_ENROLL_TIMEOUT)
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f'Enrollment failed: {e}')
        return None
    if not data.get('client_id') or not data.get('client_secret'):
        print(f'Enrollment response missing credential fields: {data}')
        return None
    credential = {'client_id': data['client_id'], 'client_secret': data['client_secret']}
    try:
        _store_credential(credential)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f'Could not store credential: {e}')
    return credential

def _get_credential():
    """This install's credential, enrolling on first use. None when that is not possible."""
    global _credential, _last_attempt
    if _credential:
        return _credential
    with _lock:
        if _credential:
            return _credential
        _credential = _read_credential()
        if _credential:
            return _credential
        if time.time() - _last_attempt < _RETRY_INTERVAL:
            return None
        _last_attempt = time.time()
        _credential = _enroll()
        return _credential

def _refresh_credential(used_id):
    """Discard the rejected credential so the next attempt enrolls a replacement."""
    global _credential, _last_attempt
    with _lock:
        if _credential and _credential['client_id'] != used_id:
            return
        _credential, _last_attempt = (None, 0.0)
        try:
            os.remove(_CREDENTIAL_FILE)
        except OSError:
            pass

def _client_meta():
    """The facts about this install that change over its life, signed with each request."""
    from config import APP_VERSION, settings
    return f"v={APP_VERSION};lang={settings.get('language') or 'unknown'}"

def _canonical_string(method, url, params, meta, timestamp, nonce):
    """The exact bytes both sides sign."""
    pairs = '\n'.join((f'{key}={params[key]}' for key in sorted(params)))
    return '\n'.join((method.upper(), urlparse(url).path, pairs, meta, timestamp, nonce))

def _signed_headers(method, url, params):
    """Shared key plus proof this install holds its secret, which is never transmitted."""
    credential = _get_credential()
    if not credential:
        raise RuntimeError('Not enrolled: this request cannot be signed')
    meta = _client_meta()
    timestamp = str(int(time.time()))
    nonce = uuid.uuid4().hex
    message = _canonical_string(method, url, params or {}, meta, timestamp, nonce)
    return {'x-api-key': CLIENT_API_KEY, 'X-Client-Id': credential['client_id'], 'X-Client-Meta': meta, 'X-Ts': timestamp, 'X-Nonce': nonce, 'X-Sig': hmac.new(credential['client_secret'].encode(), message.encode(), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()}

def signed_get(url, params, timeout):
    """GET signed as this install, enrolling again if the server no longer knows it."""
    from config import RETRY_STATUSES

    def send():
        for attempt in range(_REQUEST_ATTEMPTS):
            final_attempt = attempt == _REQUEST_ATTEMPTS - 1
            headers = _signed_headers('GET', url, params)
            try:
                response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, params=params, timeout=timeout)
                if response.status_code not in RETRY_STATUSES or final_attempt:
                    return (headers['X-Client-Id'], response)
                print(f'Signed request returned HTTP {response.status_code}, retrying')
            except (requests.ConnectionError, requests.Timeout) as e:
                if final_attempt:
                    raise
                print(f'Signed request failed, retrying: {e}')
            time.sleep(2 ** attempt + random.uniform(0, 0.5))
    used_id, response = send()
    if response.status_code == _UNKNOWN_CREDENTIAL:
        _refresh_credential(used_id)
        _, response = send()
    return response

```

`tests/i18n.test.mjs`:

```mjs
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import vm from "node:vm";
import ts from "typescript";

function loadI18n(getCurrentLanguage = async () => "english") {
  const cache = new Map();
  const effects = [];
  let notify;
  const react = {
    useEffect(effect) { effects.push(effect); },
    useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, snapshot) {
      notify = { calls: 0, snapshot };
      notify.unsubscribe = subscribe(() => { notify.calls++; });
      return snapshot();
    },
  };
  function load(name) {
    if (cache.has(name)) return cache.get(name);
    const { outputText } = ts.transpileModule(readFileSync(new URL(`../src/utils/${name}.ts`, import.meta.url), "utf8"), {
      compilerOptions: { module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2020 },
    });
    const module = { exports: {} };
    cache.set(name, module.exports);
    vm.runInNewContext(outputText, {
      module, exports: module.exports, SteamClient: { Settings: { GetCurrentLanguage: getCurrentLanguage } },
      require: name => name === "react" ? react : load(name.replace("./", "")),
    });
    return module.exports;
  }
  return { i18n: load("i18n"), effects, notification: () => notify };
}

test("Steam language identifiers distinguish Simplified and Traditional Chinese", async () => {
  const { i18n } = loadI18n();
  for (const language of ["schinese", "ZH_cn", "zh-Hans", "zh-SG"]) assert.equal(i18n.steamLocale(language), "zh-CN");
  for (const language of ["tchinese", "zh_TW", "zh-Hant", "zh-HK"]) assert.equal(i18n.steamLocale(language), "zh-TW");
  for (const language of ["english", "german", "", null, undefined, {}]) assert.equal(i18n.steamLocale(language), "en");
});

test("initial detection and later Steam language changes notify mounted views", async () => {
  let language = "schinese";
  const { i18n, notification } = loadI18n(async () => language);
  assert.equal(i18n.useTranslations().settings, "Settings");
  await i18n.initLanguage();
  assert.equal(notification().calls, 1);
  assert.equal(notification().snapshot(), "zh-CN");
  assert.equal(i18n.translations().settings, "设置");
  assert.equal(i18n.getSearchLanguage(), "zh");
  language = "tchinese";
  await i18n.initLanguage();
  assert.equal(notification().calls, 2);
  assert.equal(i18n.translations().settings, "設定");
  assert.equal(i18n.getSearchLanguage(), "zh");
  language = "english";
  await i18n.initLanguage();
  assert.equal(i18n.translations().settings, "Settings");
  assert.equal(i18n.getSearchLanguage(), "en");
  notification().unsubscribe();
});

test("concurrent detection shares a request and temporary Steam failures preserve the language", async () => {
  let calls = 0, release;
  const { i18n } = loadI18n(() => {
    calls++;
    return calls === 1 ? new Promise(resolve => { release = resolve; }) : Promise.reject(new Error("Steam unavailable"));
  });
  const first = i18n.initLanguage();
  const second = i18n.initLanguage();
  assert.equal(first, second);
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
  release("tchinese");
  await first;
  await i18n.initLanguage();
  assert.equal(i18n.getLanguage(), "zh-TW");
});

test("translated notices, errors and formatted fields use the selected script", () => {
  const { i18n } = loadI18n();
  const cn = i18n.translations("zh-CN"), tw = i18n.translations("zh-TW");
  assert.equal(i18n.errorMessage("sidecarSaved", cn), cn.sidecarSaved);
  assert.equal(i18n.errorMessage("library_changed", tw), tw.libraryChanged);
  assert.equal(i18n.errorMessage("Launch options changed elsewhere. Refresh this page and try again.", cn), cn.optionsChanged);
  assert.equal(i18n.errorMessage("unrecognized backend detail", tw), tw.unknownError);
  assert.equal(cn.downloadedTitle(3), "已下载（3）");
  assert.equal(tw.downloading(42), "下載中 · 42%");
  assert.ok(tw.deleteDescription("Game Trainer.exe").includes("Game Trainer.exe"));
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(i18n.translations("en"))) {
    assert.equal(typeof cn[key], typeof value, key);
    assert.equal(typeof tw[key], typeof value, key);
  }
});
```

`tests/menu.test.mjs`:

```mjs
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import vm from "node:vm";
import ts from "typescript";

function loadMenu(ui) {
  const source = readFileSync(new URL("../src/patch.tsx", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  const { outputText } = ts.transpileModule(source, {
    compilerOptions: { module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, jsx: ts.JsxEmit.ReactJSX },
  });
  const module = { exports: {} };
  vm.runInNewContext(outputText, {
    module, exports: module.exports,
    require(name) {
      if (name === "@decky/ui") return ui;
      if (name === "react/jsx-runtime") return { jsx: () => ({}) };
      return { menuItemLabel: () => "GCM", gamePageRoute: (id) => `/gcm/${id}` };
    },
  });
  return module.exports.default;
}

test("every nested Steam patch is removed, even after repeated renders", () => {
  class Inner {
    render() { return { props: { children: [[]] } }; }
    shouldComponentUpdate() { return true; }
  }
  const component = { _owner: { pendingProps: { overview: { appid: 123 } } }, props: { children: [] }, type: () => ({ type: Inner }) };
  class Library { render() { return component; } }
  const originals = [Library.prototype.render, component.type, Inner.prototype.render, Inner.prototype.shouldComponentUpdate];
  let count = 0;
  const install = loadMenu({
    findModuleByExport: () => ({ factory: function () { /* navigator: */ } }),
    fakeRenderComponent: () => ({ type: Library }), findInTree: () => null,
    afterPatch(target, key, callback) {
      const original = target[key];
      target[key] = function (...args) { return callback(args, original.apply(this, args)); };
      count++;
      return { unpatch() { target[key] = original; count--; } };
    },
  });
  const cleanup = install();
  new Library().render();
  component.type(); component.type();
  assert.equal(count, 4);
  cleanup(); cleanup();
  assert.equal(count, 0);
  assert.deepEqual([Library.prototype.render, component.type, Inner.prototype.render, Inner.prototype.shouldComponentUpdate], originals);
});

test("missing Steam menu leaves plugin initialization available", () => {
  const install = loadMenu({ findModuleByExport() { throw new Error("Steam changed"); } });
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => install()());
});
```

`tests/sidecar.test.mjs`:

```mjs
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import vm from "node:vm";
import ts from "typescript";

function loadDomain(steam = {}) {
  const cache = new Map();
  function load(name) {
    if (cache.has(name)) return cache.get(name);
    const source = readFileSync(new URL(`../src/domain/${name}.ts`, import.meta.url), "utf8");
    const { outputText } = ts.transpileModule(source, { compilerOptions: { module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2020 } });
    const module = { exports: {} };
    cache.set(name, module.exports);
    vm.runInNewContext(outputText, { module, exports: module.exports, require: name => load(name.replace("./", "")),
      SteamClient: { Apps: steam }, window: { setTimeout, clearTimeout } });
    return module.exports;
  }
  return { ...load("sidecar"), LaunchOptions: load("options").LaunchOptions };
}

test("sidecar paths round-trip without altering unrelated launch source", () => {
  const { editSidecar, sidecarPath } = loadDomain();
  const original = `MANGOHUD=1 gamescope -f -- %command% -windowed`;
  for (const path of ["/home/deck/Trainer.exe", `/home/deck/Trainer Folder/it's \"$trainer\".exe`, "/home/deck/游戏/Trainer.bat"]) {
    const updated = editSidecar(original, path);
    assert.equal(sidecarPath(updated), path);
    assert.ok(updated.startsWith("MANGOHUD=1 "));
    assert.ok(updated.endsWith("gamescope -f -- %command% -windowed"));
    assert.equal(sidecarPath(editSidecar(updated)), undefined);
    assert.ok(editSidecar(updated).startsWith("MANGOHUD=1 "));
    assert.ok(editSidecar(updated).endsWith("gamescope -f -- %command% -windowed"));
  }
});

test("existing shared folders survive enabling and disabling sidecars", () => {
  const { editSidecar, LaunchOptions } = loadDomain();
  const original = `PRESSURE_VESSEL_FILESYSTEMS_RW='/mnt/tools:/mnt/mods' %command%`;
  const updated = editSidecar(original, "/home/deck/Trainer.exe");
  assert.equal(LaunchOptions.parse(updated).getEnvironment("PRESSURE_VESSEL_FILESYSTEMS_RW"), "/mnt/tools:/mnt/mods:/home/deck");
  assert.equal(LaunchOptions.parse(editSidecar(updated)).getEnvironment("PRESSURE_VESSEL_FILESYSTEMS_RW"), "/mnt/tools:/mnt/mods:/home/deck");
});

test("complex launch source and dynamic filesystem values are never overwritten", () => {
  const { editSidecar } = loadDomain();
  assert.throws(() => editSidecar("%command% && other", "/home/deck/Trainer.exe"), /safely/);
  assert.throws(() => editSidecar("PRESSURE_VESSEL_FILESYSTEMS_RW=$HOME %command%", "/home/deck/Trainer.exe"), /dynamic/);
  assert.throws(() => editSidecar("", "/home/deck/bad\nname.exe"), /valid/);
});

test("saving refuses stale state and unregisters its Steam subscription", async () => {
  let writes = 0, unregisters = 0;
  const { saveSidecar } = loadDomain({
    RegisterForAppDetails(_id, callback) {
      queueMicrotask(() => callback({ strLaunchOptions: "NEW=1 %command%" }));
      return { unregister() { unregisters++; } };
    },
    SetAppLaunchOptions() { writes++; },
  });
  await assert.rejects(() => saveSidecar("42", "%command%", "/home/deck/Trainer.exe"), /changed elsewhere/);
  assert.equal(writes, 0);
  assert.equal(unregisters, 1);
});
```

```py
from __future__ import annotations

    "translations.json": [
        {"en_US": "Elden Ring", "zh_CN": "艾尔登法环"},
        {"en_US": "Black Myth: Wukong", "zh_CN": "黑神话：悟空"},

def write_fixture_db():
    shutil.rmtree(DATABASE_PATH, ignore_errors=True)
    os.makedirs(DATABASE_PATH)
    for name, content in FIXTURE_DB.items():
        with open(os.path.join(DATABASE_PATH, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(content, f, ensure_ascii=False)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

`tests/test_gcm_core.py`:

```py
"""Protocol, generation and real HTTP integration tests with no production key."""
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import hashlib
import hmac
import http.server
import importlib.util
import io
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path[:0] = [str(ROOT / "tests"), str(ROOT / "py_modules"), str(ROOT)]
import main
import decky
import data_source
import gcm_auth_adapter
import gcm_auth_platform
import gcm_transport
import http_download

FIXTURE = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "auth_config.py"


def response(status=200, data=None):
    result = Mock(status_code=status)
    result.json.return_value = data or {}
    if status >= 400:
        import requests
        result.raise_for_status.side_effect = requests.HTTPError(f"HTTP {status}", response=result)
    return result


class AuthTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temporary.name)
        for target, value in (("decky.DECKY_PLUGIN_SETTINGS_DIR", str(self.root)),
                              ("data_source.SECRET_CONFIG_PATHS", (str(FIXTURE),)),
                              ("data_source.BUNDLED_AUTH_MODULE", "_gcm_core.no_test_auth")):
            patcher = patch(target, value)
            patcher.start()
            self.addCleanup(patcher.stop)
        data_source.reset()
        self.addCleanup(data_source.reset)
        self.auth = data_source.client()

    def test_platform_adapter_uses_linux_and_persists_enrollment(self):
        get = Mock(side_effect=[response(data={"client_id": "test-device", "client_secret": "test-secret"}), response()])
        with patch.object(gcm_transport, "get", get):
            self.auth.signed_get("https://example.invalid/download", {"filePath": "GCM/Data/translations.json"}, (1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(get.call_args_list[0].kwargs["params"]["platform"], "linux")
        self.assertEqual(get.call_args_list[0].kwargs["params"]["app"], "GCM")
        self.assertEqual(len(get.call_args_list[0].kwargs["params"]["machineHash"]), 32)
        self.assertEqual(gcm_auth_platform.machine_hash(), gcm_auth_platform.machine_hash())
        self.assertEqual(gcm_auth_platform.read_credential()["client_id"], "test-device")
        another = gcm_auth_adapter.load(str(FIXTURE))
        with patch.object(gcm_transport, "get", return_value=response()) as get_again:
            another.signed_get("https://example.invalid/download", {}, (1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(get_again.call_count, 1)  # Reuse disk credential after restart.
        if os.name != "nt":
            self.assertEqual(Path(gcm_auth_platform.credential_path()).stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)

    def test_signature_matches_protocol(self):
        self.auth._credential = {"client_id": "test-device", "client_secret": "test-secret"}
        with patch.object(self.auth.time, "time", return_value=1000), patch.object(self.auth.uuid, "uuid4", return_value=SimpleNamespace(hex="test-nonce")):
            headers = self.auth._signed_headers("GET", "https://example.invalid/download", {"z": "last", "a": "first"})
        canonical = "\n".join(("GET", "/download", "a=first\nz=last", headers["X-Client-Meta"], "1000", "test-nonce"))
        expected = hmac.new(b"test-secret", canonical.encode(), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
        self.assertEqual(headers["X-Sig"], expected)


    def test_401_reenrolls_and_retries_with_new_device(self):
        old = {"client_id": "old-device", "client_secret": "old-secret"}
        new = {"client_id": "new-device", "client_secret": "new-secret"}
        get = Mock(side_effect=[response(data=old), response(401), response(data=new), response()])
        with patch.object(gcm_transport, "get", get):
            result = self.auth.signed_get("https://example.invalid/download", {}, (1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(result.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(get.call_count, 4)
        self.assertEqual(get.call_args_list[1].kwargs["headers"]["X-Client-Id"], "old-device")
        self.assertEqual(get.call_args_list[3].kwargs["headers"]["X-Client-Id"], "new-device")
        self.assertEqual(gcm_auth_platform.read_credential(), new)


    def test_enrollment_connection_failure_has_safe_structured_error(self):
        import requests
        with patch("requests.Session.request", side_effect=requests.Timeout("https://private.invalid/?secret=hidden")), contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as output:
            with self.assertRaises(data_source.DataSourceUnavailable) as ctx:
                data_source.get_signed_download_url("GCM/Data/translations.json")
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "connection_failed")
        self.assertNotIn("private.invalid", str(ctx.exception) + output.getvalue())
        self.assertEqual(data_source.status()["state"], "connection_failed")

    def test_registration_server_failure_and_cooldown_keep_server_status(self):
        from requests import Response
        failure = Response()
        failure.status_code = 503
        failure._content = b"{}"
        with patch("requests.Session.request", return_value=failure) as request:
            for _ in range(2):
                with self.assertRaises(data_source.DataSourceUnavailable) as ctx:
                    data_source.get_signed_download_url("GCM/Data/translations.json")
                self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "server_error")
        self.assertEqual(request.call_count, 1)


    def test_generation_with_and_without_secret_cannot_reuse_stale_key(self):
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("build_gcm_core", ROOT / "scripts" / "build_gcm_core.py")
        builder = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(builder)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            builder.validate("MISSING_GLOBAL\n", "broken.py")
        output = self.root / "generated"
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
            builder.build(output, FIXTURE)
            self.assertTrue((output / "auth.py").is_file())
            builder.build(output)
        self.assertFalse((output / "auth.py").exists())
        for name in ("config.py", "search_index.py", "download.py", "search.py"):
            compile((output / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), name, "exec")


class DownloadTest(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.payload = bytes(range(256)) * (12 * 1024)  # > 2 MB triggers upstream range workers.
        cls.hits = []
        class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass

            def do_GET(self):
                cls.hits.append((self.path, self.headers.get("Range"), self.headers.get("If-None-Match")))
                payload = b'[ {"game_name": "Example"} ]' if self.path == "/db.json" else cls.payload
                if self.path == "/invalid.json":
                    payload = b"not json"
                if self.headers.get("If-None-Match") == '"' + hashlib.md5(payload).hexdigest() + '"':
                    self.send_response(304)
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                start, end = 0, len(payload)-1
                requested = self.headers.get("Range")
                if requested:
                    start, end = map(int, requested.removeprefix("bytes=").split("-"))
                    self.send_response(206)
                    self.send_header("Content-Range", f"bytes {start}-{end}/{len(payload)}")
                else:
                    self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(end-start+1))
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    self.wfile.write(payload[start:end+1])
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError, ConnectionAbortedError):
                    pass
        cls.server = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
        cls.thread = threading.Thread(target=cls.server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        cls.thread.start()
        cls.origin = f"http://127.0.0.1:{cls.server.server_port}"

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        cls.server.shutdown()
        cls.server.server_close()
        cls.thread.join()

    def test_parallel_download_and_304_cache(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            progress = []
            path = http_download.download(self.origin + "/trainer.exe", directory, lambda done,total: progress.append((done,total)))
            self.assertEqual(Path(path).read_bytes(), self.payload)
            self.assertTrue(any(hit[0] == "/trainer.exe" and hit[1] for hit in self.hits))
            self.assertEqual(progress[-1], (len(self.payload), len(self.payload)))
            again = http_download.download(self.origin + "/trainer.exe", directory)
            self.assertEqual(again, path)
            self.assertIsNotNone(self.hits[-1][2])


    def test_bad_database_preserves_previous_copy(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            path = http_download.download(self.origin + "/db.json", directory, filename="database.json")
            previous = Path(path).read_bytes()
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                http_download.download(self.origin + "/invalid.json", directory, filename="database.json")
            self.assertEqual(Path(path).read_bytes(), previous)
            self.assertEqual(sorted(p.name for p in Path(directory).iterdir()), ["database.json"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`tests/test_library.py`:

```py
"""Library and installation integration tests, isolated from user files/network."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import io
import stat
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

TESTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
for path in (TESTS, TESTS.parent / "py_modules", TESTS.parent):
    sys.path.insert(0, str(path))

import main
import trainer_archive
import trainer_download
import trainer_library
from test_backend import write_fixture_db


def zip_bytes(files: dict[str, bytes]) -> bytes:
    out = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(out, "w") as archive:
        for name, data in files.items():
            archive.writestr(name, data)
    return out.getvalue()


class LibraryTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)
        self.library = self.root / "trainers"
        self.library.mkdir()
        settings = patch.object(trainer_library, "SETTINGS_PATH", str(self.root / "settings.json"))
        settings.start()
        self.addCleanup(settings.stop)
        trainer_library.set_directory(str(self.library))
        write_fixture_db()
        trainer_download._jobs.clear()

    def test_directory_persists_and_switch_does_not_move_files(self):
        (self.library / "trainer.CT").write_bytes(b"table")
        target = self.root / "other"
        target.mkdir()
        trainer_library.set_directory(str(target))
        self.assertEqual(Path(trainer_library.directory()), target.resolve())
        self.assertTrue((self.library / "trainer.CT").is_file())
        self.assertEqual(trainer_library.scan()["trainers"], [])
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "library_changed"):
            trainer_download.start({}, expected_directory=str(self.library))
        with self.assertRaises((OSError, ValueError)):
            trainer_library.set_directory(str(self.root / "missing"))
        self.assertEqual(Path(trainer_library.directory()), target.resolve())

    def test_import_copies_and_binds_without_replacing_source(self):
        source = self.root / "Trainer.exe"
        source.write_bytes(b"program")
        first = trainer_library.import_file(str(source), "42", "Example", str(self.library))
        second = trainer_library.import_file(str(source), "42", "Example", str(self.library))
        self.assertNotEqual(first["path"], second["path"])
        self.assertEqual(source.read_bytes(), b"program")
        entries = trainer_library.scan("42", "Unrelated name")["trainers"]
        self.assertEqual(len(entries), 2)
        self.assertEqual(trainer_library.scan("43", "Example")["trainers"], [])
        trainer_library.delete(entries[0]["id"], str(self.library), "42")
        self.assertEqual(len(trainer_library.scan()["trainers"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(source.read_bytes(), b"program")

    def test_import_failure_does_not_publish_partial_installation(self):
        source = self.root / "Trainer.exe"
        source.write_bytes(b"program")
        with patch("trainer_library.shutil.copy2", side_effect=OSError("copy failed")):
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                trainer_library.import_file(str(source), "42", "Example", str(self.library))
        self.assertEqual(list(self.library.iterdir()), [])

    def test_import_cleanup_failure_does_not_report_committed_copy_as_failed(self):
        source = self.root / "Trainer.exe"
        source.write_bytes(b"program")
        real_temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory

        class FailingCleanup(real_temporary):
            def __exit__(self, *args):
                super().__exit__(*args)
                raise OSError("cleanup failed")

        with patch("trainer_library.tempfile.TemporaryDirectory", FailingCleanup):
            result = trainer_library.import_file(str(source), "42", "Example", str(self.library))
        self.assertTrue((Path(result["path"]) / "Trainer.exe").is_file())
        self.assertEqual(len(trainer_library.scan("42")["trainers"]), 1)

    def test_management_rejects_stale_directory_and_unknown_files(self):
        source = self.root / "Trainer.exe"
        source.write_bytes(b"program")
        trainer_library.import_file(str(source), "42", "Example", str(self.library))
        item = trainer_library.scan()["trainers"][0]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "library_changed"):
            trainer_library.delete(item["id"], str(self.root), "42")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Unknown trainer file"):
            trainer_library.executable(item["id"], "../Trainer.exe", str(self.library))
        self.assertTrue(Path(trainer_library.executable(item["id"], "Trainer.exe", str(self.library))).is_file())

    def test_unmanaged_folder_and_shared_trainer_are_not_deleted(self):
        folder = self.library / "Existing"
        folder.mkdir()
        (folder / "Trainer.exe").write_bytes(b"keep")
        item = trainer_library.scan()["trainers"][0]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Only trainers"):
            trainer_library.delete(item["id"], str(self.library), "42")
        trainer_library.write_json(folder / trainer_library.METADATA, {"schema_version": 1, "app_ids": ["42", "43"]})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "shared"):
            trainer_library.delete(item["id"], str(self.library), "42")
        self.assertTrue((folder / "Trainer.exe").exists())

    def test_cancel_download_checks_game_and_signals_worker(self):
        token = threading.Event()
        trainer_download._jobs["cancel"] = {"id": "cancel", "state": "downloading", "app_ids": ["42"]}
        trainer_download._cancel["cancel"] = token
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                trainer_download.cancel("cancel", "43")
            self.assertFalse(token.is_set())
            self.assertEqual(trainer_download.cancel("cancel", "42")["state"], "downloading")
            self.assertTrue(token.is_set())
        finally:
            trainer_download._cancel.pop("cancel", None)

    def test_cancelling_during_progress_cleans_files_and_never_publishes(self):
        def download(url, directory, callback):
            Path(directory).mkdir(parents=True)
            (Path(directory) / "partial.exe").write_bytes(b"partial")
            trainer_download.cancel(job["id"], "42")
            callback(7, 100)
            self.fail("A cancelled worker must stop at its next progress callback.")

        async def run():
            nonlocal job
            job = trainer_download.start({"id": "cancel-active", "trainer_name": "Test", "url": "test"}, app_id="42")
            await asyncio.gather(*trainer_download._tasks)

        job = None
        with patch("trainer_download.data_source.get_signed_download_url", return_value="https://example.test"), patch("trainer_download.http_download.download", side_effect=download):
            asyncio.run(run())
        self.assertEqual(job["state"], "cancelled")
        self.assertIsNone(job["path"])
        self.assertEqual(list(self.library.iterdir()), [])


    def _install(self, filename="trainer.zip", payload=None, app_id="1245620", switch=False):
        payload = payload if payload is not None else zip_bytes({"v1/Trainer.exe": b"trainer", "instructions.txt": b"help"})
        trainer = {"id": "example", "trainer_name": "[FL] Elden Ring Trainer", "game_name": "Elden Ring",
                   "origin": "fling_main", "version": "1.0", "url": "catalog/trainer.zip"}

        def download(url, directory, callback):
            Path(directory).mkdir(parents=True)
            path = Path(directory) / filename
            path.write_bytes(payload)
            callback(len(payload), len(payload))
            return str(path)

        async def run():
            job = trainer_download.start(trainer, app_id=app_id)
            if switch:
                other = self.root / "new-library"
                other.mkdir()
                trainer_library.set_directory(str(other))
            await asyncio.gather(*trainer_download._tasks)
            return job

        with patch("trainer_download.data_source.get_signed_download_url", return_value="https://example.test/trainer"), patch("trainer_download.http_download.download", side_effect=download):
            return asyncio.run(run())

    def test_download_extracts_and_records_game_after_restart(self):
        job = self._install()
        self.assertEqual(job["state"], "done", job["error"])
        self.assertTrue((Path(job["path"]) / "v1" / "Trainer.exe").is_file())
        self.assertFalse(list(Path(job["path"]).glob("*.zip")))
        trainer_download._jobs.clear()  # The disk record must survive a plugin reload.
        local = trainer_library.scan("1245620", "Changed Name")["trainers"]
        self.assertEqual(len(local), 1)
        self.assertEqual(local[0]["trainer_id"], "example")
        self.assertEqual(trainer_library.scan("999", "Elden Ring")["trainers"], [])

    def test_direct_ct_download_is_added_without_extraction(self):
        job = self._install("game.CT", b"<CheatTable/>")
        self.assertEqual(job["state"], "done", job["error"])
        self.assertTrue((Path(job["path"]) / "game.CT").is_file())

    def test_switching_folder_during_download_keeps_original_destination(self):
        job = self._install(switch=True)
        self.assertEqual(job["state"], "done", job["error"])
        self.assertEqual(Path(job["path"]).parent, self.library.resolve())
        self.assertEqual(trainer_library.scan()["trainers"], [])

    def test_failed_extraction_cleans_staging_without_touching_existing_files(self):
        existing = self.library / "existing.CT"
        existing.write_bytes(b"keep")
        job = self._install(payload=zip_bytes({"../escape.exe": b"bad"}))
        self.assertEqual(job["state"], "error")
        self.assertIsNone(job["path"])
        self.assertEqual(existing.read_bytes(), b"keep")
        self.assertEqual([p.name for p in self.library.iterdir()], ["existing.CT"])
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "escape.exe").exists())


    def test_cleanup_failure_after_commit_keeps_install_successful(self):
        real_temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory
        class FailingCleanup(real_temporary):
            def __exit__(self, *args):
                super().__exit__(*args)
                raise OSError("cleanup failed")
        with patch("trainer_download.tempfile.TemporaryDirectory", FailingCleanup):
            job = self._install()
        self.assertEqual(job["state"], "done")
        self.assertEqual(len(trainer_library.scan()["trainers"]), 1)

    def test_shutdown_waits_for_worker_and_prevents_publication(self):
        started, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        def download(url, directory, progress):
            started.set()
            if not release.wait(3):
                raise RuntimeError("test timeout")
            Path(directory).mkdir(parents=True)
            target = Path(directory) / "trainer.CT"
            target.write_bytes(b"table")
            return str(target)
        async def run():
            job = trainer_download.start({"id": "stop", "trainer_name": "Stop", "url": "test"})
            self.assertTrue(await asyncio.to_thread(started.wait, 2))
            stopping = asyncio.create_task(trainer_download.shutdown())
            await asyncio.sleep(0)
            self.assertFalse(stopping.done())
            release.set()
            await stopping
            return job
        try:
            with patch("trainer_download.data_source.get_signed_download_url", return_value="https://example.test"), patch("trainer_download.http_download.download", side_effect=download):
                job = asyncio.run(run())
            self.assertEqual(job["state"], "cancelled")
            self.assertEqual(list(self.library.iterdir()), [])
        finally:
            release.set()
            trainer_download.resume()

    def test_corrupt_metadata_does_not_hide_other_trainers(self):
        folder = self.library / "Elden Ring Trainer"
        folder.mkdir()
        (folder / "trainer.exe").write_bytes(b"trainer")
        trainer_library.write_json(folder / trainer_library.METADATA, {
            "app_ids": 1245620, "game_name": ["wrong type"], "trainer_name": 42,
        })
        self.assertEqual(len(trainer_library.scan("1245620", "Elden Ring")["trainers"]), 1)

    def test_search_in_second_panel_keeps_first_panel_downloadable(self):
        plugin = main.Plugin()
        async def run():
            first = await plugin.search_trainers("elden", "en")
            await plugin.search_trainers("wukong", "en")
            with patch("main.trainer_download.start", return_value={"id": "job"}):
                return await plugin.download_trainer(first["results"][0]["id"], "1245620")
        self.assertTrue(asyncio.run(run())["success"])


class ArchiveTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_rejects_absolute_windows_paths_and_links(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            for name in ("/escape.exe", "C:\\escape.exe", "..\\escape.exe"):
                with self.subTest(name=name):
                    archive = root / "input.zip"
                    archive.write_bytes(zip_bytes({name: b"bad"}))
                    with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                        trainer_archive.extract(str(archive), str(root / "output"))
            archive = root / "link.zip"
            with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, "w") as out:
                entry = zipfile.ZipInfo("link.exe")
                entry.create_system = 3
                entry.external_attr = (stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777) << 16
                out.writestr(entry, "../../outside")
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                trainer_archive.extract(str(archive), str(root / "output"))

    def test_enforces_expanded_size_limit(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            archive = root / "input.zip"
            archive.write_bytes(zip_bytes({"large.exe": b"123456789"}))
            with patch.object(trainer_archive, "MAX_BYTES", 4), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                trainer_archive.extract(str(archive), str(root / "output"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```
